--- FILE: public/sw.js ---
self.addEventListener("install", () => self.skipWaiting());
self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim()));

self.addEventListener("push", function (event) {
  let data = {};
  try {
    data = event.data ? event.data.json() : {};
  } catch (e) {
    data = { title: "Menu", body: event.data ? event.data.text() : "New update" };
  }

  const options = {
    body: data.body || "New update from Menu",
    icon: "/icon-192.png",
    badge: "/icon-192.png",
    vibrate: [200, 100, 200, 100, 200],
    data: { url: "/inbox" }, // Always open inbox
  };

  event.waitUntil(
    self.registration.showNotification(data.title || "Menu", options)
  );
});

self.addEventListener("notificationclick", function (event) {
  event.notification.close();
  event.waitUntil(
    clients.matchAll({ type: "window" }).then((clientList) => {
      for (const client of clientList) {
        if (client.url.includes(self.location.origin) && "focus" in client) {
          client.navigate("/inbox");
          return client.focus();
        }
      }
      if (clients.openWindow) return clients.openWindow("/inbox");
    })
  );
});
--- END FILE: public/sw.js ---

--- FILE: scripts/extract-ramadan.js ---
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const src = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "..", "ramadan.liquid"), "utf8");

function extractArray(name) {
  // Match: {%- assign NAME = "..." | split: "|" -%}
  const re = new RegExp(
    `assign\\s+${name}\\s*=\\s*"([\\s\\S]*?)"\\s*\\|\\s*split:\\s*"\\|"`,
    "m"
  );
  const m = src.match(re);
  if (!m) {
    console.warn(`  ⚠️  Could not find array: ${name}`);
    return [];
  }
  return m[1].split("|").map((s) => s.trim());
}

const fields = [
  "titles",
  "titles_ar",
  "types",
  "sides",
  "sides_ar",
  "drinks",
  "drinks_ar",
  "ingredients",
  "ingredients_ar",
  "methods",
  "methods_ar",
  "shopping",
  "shopping_ar",
];

const data = {};
for (const f of fields) {
  data[f] = extractArray(f);
  console.log(`${f}: ${data[f].length} entries`);
}

// Sanity check lengths
const expected = 30;
for (const f of ["titles", "titles_ar", "types", "sides", "sides_ar", "drinks", "drinks_ar", "ingredients", "ingredients_ar", "methods", "methods_ar"]) {
  if (data[f].length !== expected) {
    console.warn(`  ⚠️  ${f} has ${data[f].length} entries — expected ${expected}`);
  }
}

// Build a recipes array ready for the API
const recipes = [];
for (let i = 0; i < expected; i++) {
  recipes.push({
    name: data.titles[i] || "",
    nameAr: data.titles_ar[i] || "",
    description: data.sides[i] || "",
    descriptionAr: data.sides_ar[i] || "",
    cuisine: "Middle Eastern",
    cuisineAr: "شرق أوسطي",
    prepTime: 30,
    cookTime: 60,
    servings: 4,
    dietaryTags: mapTypeToTag(data.types[i]),
    imageUrl: null,
    videoUrl: null,
    ingredients: parseIngredientList(data.ingredients[i] || "", data.ingredients_ar[i] || ""),
    instructions: splitInstructions(data.methods[i] || "", data.methods_ar[i] || ""),
  });
}

function mapTypeToTag(t) {
  const map = {
    meat: [],
    chicken: [],
    seafood: [],
    veggie: ["Vegetarian"],
  };
  return map[t] || [];
}

// Split "a, b, c" style ingredient lists into rows.
// Arabic uses "،" as a comma separator.
function parseIngredientList(en, ar) {
  const enParts = en.split(/,\s*/).filter(Boolean);
  const arParts = ar.split(/،\s*|\s*,\s*/).filter(Boolean);
  const len = Math.max(enParts.length, arParts.length);
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    const enItem = (enParts[i] || "").trim();
    const arItem = (arParts[i] || "").trim();
    if (!enItem && !arItem) continue;

    // Try to peel off parenthesized quantity: "Basmati rice (3 cups)"
    const { name, amount } = splitAmount(enItem || arItem);

    out.push({
      name,
      nameAr: arItem ? splitAmount(arItem).name : "",
      amount: amount || "",
      unit: "",
      unitAr: "",
      preparation: "",
      preparationAr: "",
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function splitAmount(s) {
  const m = s.match(/^(.*?)\s*\(([^)]+)\)\s*$/);
  if (m) return { name: m[1].trim(), amount: m[2].trim() };
  return { name: s.trim(), amount: "" };
}

// Split instruction text into sentences -> steps.
function splitInstructions(en, ar) {
  const enSentences = en.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const arSentences = ar.split(/(?<=[.!؟])\s+/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const len = Math.max(enSentences.length, arSentences.length);
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    out.push({
      text: enSentences[i] || "",
      textAr: arSentences[i] || "",
    });
  }
  return out;
}

fs.writeFileSync(
  path.join(__dirname, "ramadan-recipes.json"),
  JSON.stringify(recipes, null, 2),
  "utf8"
);

console.log(`\n✅ Wrote ${recipes.length} recipes to scripts/ramadan-recipes.json`);
--- END FILE: scripts/extract-ramadan.js ---

--- FILE: scripts/import-lebanese.js ---
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const target = process.argv[2] || "http://127.0.0.1:3000";
const filePath = path.join(__dirname, "lebanese-recipes.json");

if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
  console.error(`❌ File not found: ${filePath}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8"));
const list = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw.lebanese_recipes || raw.recipes || [raw];

if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) {
  console.error("❌ No recipes found in the file");
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log(`Found ${list.length} recipes\n`);

function parseServings(s) {
  if (!s) return 4;
  const str = String(s);
  if (/not specified|غير محدد/i.test(str)) return 4;
  const m = str.match(/(\d+)/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : 4;
}

function parseMinutes(s) {
  if (!s) return 0;
  const str = String(s).toLowerCase();
  if (/not specified|غير محدد/.test(str)) return 0;
  let total = 0;
  const h = str.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hour|hr|h\b|ساعة)/);
  if (h) total += Math.round(parseFloat(h[1]) * 60);
  const m = str.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:min|minute|دقيقة|دقائق)/);
  if (m) total += parseInt(m[1], 10);
  return total || 0;
}

function splitAmountUnit(s) {
  if (!s) return { amount: "", unit: "", name: "" };
  if (typeof s !== "string") return { amount: "", unit: "", name: String(s) };
  const m = s.match(/^([½¼¾⅓⅔⅛⅜⅝⅞]|\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?(?:\s*[-–]\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)?|\d+\/\d+)\s*([a-zA-Z\u0600-\u06FF]+)?\s*(.*)$/);
  if (!m) return { amount: "", unit: "", name: s };
  return { amount: (m[1] || "").trim(), unit: (m[2] || "").trim(), name: (m[3] || "").trim() };
}

/**
 * Accepts ingredients in either shape:
 *   - String:  "250g fine brown burghul"
 *   - Object:  { name, nameAr, amount, unit, unitAr, preparation, preparationAr }
 */
function makeIngredient(en, ar) {
  // Object shape — pass through directly
  if (typeof en === "object" && en !== null) {
    return {
      name: en.name || en.name_en || "",
      nameAr: en.nameAr || en.name_ar || "",
      amount: String(en.amount || ""),
      unit: en.unit || en.unit_en || "",
      unitAr: en.unitAr || en.unit_ar || "",
      preparation: en.preparation || en.notes || "",
      preparationAr: en.preparationAr || en.preparation_ar || "",
    };
  }
  // String shape — parse
  const e = splitAmountUnit(en);
  const a = splitAmountUnit(ar);
  return {
    name: e.name || a.name || "",
    nameAr: a.name || "",
    amount: e.amount || a.amount || "",
    unit: e.unit || "",
    unitAr: a.unit || "",
    preparation: "",
    preparationAr: "",
  };
}

function flattenIngredients(ing, ingAr) {
  const out = [];

  // Array of objects OR array of strings
  if (Array.isArray(ing)) {
    const ar = Array.isArray(ingAr) ? ingAr : [];
    const len = Math.max(ing.length, ar.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      const e = ing[i];
      const a = ar[i];
      if (e == null && a == null) continue;
      if (typeof e === "object" && e !== null) {
        out.push(makeIngredient(e, a));
      } else {
        const es = (e || "").toString().trim();
        const as = (a || "").toString().trim();
        if (es || as) out.push(makeIngredient(es, as));
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  // Sectioned object: { base: [...], base_arabic: [...] }
  if (ing && typeof ing === "object") {
    for (const sec of Object.keys(ing).filter((k) => !k.endsWith("_arabic"))) {
      const en = ing[sec] || [];
      const ar = ing[sec + "_arabic"] || [];
      const len = Math.max(en.length, ar.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        const e = en[i];
        const a = ar[i];
        if (e == null && a == null) continue;
        if (typeof e === "object" && e !== null) {
          out.push(makeIngredient(e, a));
        } else {
          const es = (e || "").toString().trim();
          const as = (a || "").toString().trim();
          if (es || as) out.push(makeIngredient(es, as));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return out;
}

const recipes = list.map((r) => {
  const totalTime =
    Number(r.prepTime) || Number(r.prep_time_minutes) || parseMinutes(r.preparation_time) || 0;
  const cookTime =
    Number(r.cookTime) || Number(r.cook_time_minutes) || 0;
  const servings =
    Number(r.servings) || parseServings(r.servings);

  return {
    name: r.name || r.name_en || r.title_en || r.title || r.name_arabic || r.nameAr || "",
    nameAr: r.nameAr || r.name_arabic || r.title || "",
    description: r.description || r.description_en || "",
    descriptionAr: r.descriptionAr || r.description_arabic || (r.title ? r.description : "") || "",
    cuisine: r.cuisine || "Lebanese",
    cuisineAr: r.cuisineAr || "لبناني",
    category: r.category || "Main Dish",
    categoryAr: r.categoryAr || r.category_ar || "",
    servedWith: r.servedWith || r.served_with || "",
    servedWithAr: r.servedWithAr || r.served_with_ar || "",
    howToServe: r.howToServe || r.how_to_serve || "",
    howToServeAr: r.howToServeAr || r.how_to_serve_ar || "",
    prepTime: totalTime,
    cookTime,
    servings,
    dietaryTags: r.dietaryTags || r.dietary_tags || [],
    imageUrl: r.imageUrl || r.image_url || null,
    videoUrl: r.videoUrl || r.video_url || null,
    ingredients: flattenIngredients(r.ingredients, r.ingredients_arabic || r.ingredientsAr),
    instructions: (r.instructions || r.preparation || []).map((s) => {
      if (typeof s === "string") return { text: s, textAr: "" };
      return {
        text: s.text || s.text_en || s.step || "",
        textAr: s.textAr || s.text_ar || "",
      };
    }),
  };
});

console.log(`Sending ${recipes.length} recipes to ${target}/api/recipes/bulk ...`);

fetch(`${target}/api/recipes/bulk`, {
  method: "POST",
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify(recipes),
})
  .then((r) => r.json().then((d) => ({ status: r.status, data: d })))
  .then(({ status, data }) => {
    if (status >= 400) {
      console.error("❌ Failed:", status, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.log("✅ Succeeded:", data);
  })
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error("❌ Network:", err.message);
    process.exit(1);
  });
--- END FILE: scripts/import-lebanese.js ---

--- FILE: scripts/import-to-db.js ---
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const target = process.argv[2] || "http://localhost:3000";
const file = path.join(__dirname, "ramadan-recipes.json");

const recipes = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
console.log(`Posting ${recipes.length} recipes to ${target}/api/recipes/bulk ...`);

fetch(`${target}/api/recipes/bulk`, {
  method: "POST",
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify(recipes),
})
  .then((r) => r.json().then((data) => ({ status: r.status, data })))
  .then(({ status, data }) => {
    if (status >= 400) {
      console.error("❌ Import failed:", status, data);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.log("✅ Import succeeded:", data);
  })
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error("❌ Network error:", err.message);
    process.exit(1);
  });
--- END FILE: scripts/import-to-db.js ---

--- FILE: src/app/admin/page.tsx ---
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import AdminSender from "@/components/AdminSender";

async function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) redirect("/login");
  return <>{children}</>;
}

function Loading() {
  return <div className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>Loading...</div>;
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading />}>
      <AuthGate>
        <AdminSender />
      </AuthGate>
    </Suspense>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/admin/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createToken, COOKIE_NAME, COOKIE_MAX_AGE } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const { username, password } = await req.json();
  const expectedUser = process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME;
  const expectedPass = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD;

  if (!expectedUser || !expectedPass) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Admin credentials not configured" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }

  if (username !== expectedUser || password !== expectedPass) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid credentials" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, createToken(), {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "lax",
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: COOKIE_MAX_AGE,
  });
  return res;
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { COOKIE_NAME } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST() {
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, "", { path: "/", maxAge: 0 });
  return res;
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/notifications/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function DELETE(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const numericId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    await db.delete(notifications).where(eq(notifications.id, numericId));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to delete" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/notifications/[id]/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/notifications/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const list = await db.select().from(notifications).orderBy(desc(notifications.createdAt));
    return NextResponse.json(list);
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/send/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import webpush from "web-push";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions, notifications } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

function initWebPush() {
  const subject = process.env.VAPID_SUBJECT;
  const publicKey = process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY;
  const privateKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
  if (!subject || !publicKey || !privateKey) throw new Error("Missing VAPID env vars");
  webpush.setVapidDetails(subject, publicKey, privateKey);
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  
  try {
    initWebPush();
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();

    const title = body.title || "Menu";
    const text = body.body || "New recipe added";
    const url = body.url || "/inbox";

    let previewImage = null;
    let previewTitle = null;

    // Only fetch preview if a valid URL is provided
    if (url.startsWith("http")) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(url);
        const html = await res.text();
        const imgMatch = html.match(/<meta\s+property="og:image"\s+content="([^"]+)"/i);
        if (imgMatch) previewImage = imgMatch[1];
        const titleMatch = html.match(/<meta\s+property="og:title"\s+content="([^"]+)"/i);
        if (titleMatch) previewTitle = titleMatch[1].replace(/&#x27;/g, "'").replace(/&quot;/g, '"');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch link preview:", err);
      }
    }

    await db.insert(notifications).values({ title, body: text, url, previewImage, previewTitle });

    const payload = JSON.stringify({ title, body: text, url: "/inbox" });
    const subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions);
    
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      subs.map((s) => webpush.sendNotification({ endpoint: s.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: s.p256dh, auth: s.auth } }, payload))
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
      const r = results[i];
      if (r.status === "rejected" && (r.reason as any)?.statusCode === 410) {
        await db.delete(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.endpoint, subs[i].endpoint));
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ sent: results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled").length, total: subs.length });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Send error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/send/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/subscribe/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();
    
    // The frontend sends { subscription: sub }, so we unwrap it here
    const sub = body.subscription || body;

    if (!sub?.endpoint || !sub?.keys?.p256dh || !sub?.keys?.auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid subscription data" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await db
      .insert(pushSubscriptions)
      .values({
        endpoint: sub.endpoint,
        p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh,
        auth: sub.keys.auth,
      })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: pushSubscriptions.endpoint,
        set: { p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh, auth: sub.keys.auth },
      });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Subscribe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save subscription" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/subscribe/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/unsubscribe/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const { endpoint } = await req.json();

    if (!endpoint) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "endpoint required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await db
      .delete(pushSubscriptions)
      .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.endpoint, endpoint));

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Unsubscribe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/unsubscribe/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

type Payload = {
  name: string; nameAr: string;
  description: string; descriptionAr: string;
  cuisine: string; cuisineAr: string;
  category: string; categoryAr: string;
  servedWith: string; servedWithAr: string;
  howToServe: string; howToServeAr: string;
  prepTime: number; cookTime: number; servings: number;
  dietaryTags: string[]; imageUrl: string | null; videoUrl: string | null;
  ingredients: { name: string; nameAr: string; amount: string; unit: string; unitAr: string; preparation: string; preparationAr: string }[];
  instructions: { text: string; textAr: string }[];
};

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const body: Payload = await req.json();
    if (!body.name?.trim() && !body.nameAr?.trim()) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe name is required" }, { status: 400 });

    await db.update(recipes).set({
      name: body.name?.trim() || body.nameAr?.trim() || "",
      nameAr: body.nameAr?.trim() || null,
      description: body.description?.trim() || null,
      descriptionAr: body.descriptionAr?.trim() || null,
      cuisine: body.cuisine?.trim() || null,
      cuisineAr: body.cuisineAr?.trim() || null,
      category: body.category?.trim() || null,
      categoryAr: body.categoryAr?.trim() || null,
      servedWith: body.servedWith?.trim() || null,
      servedWithAr: body.servedWithAr?.trim() || null,
      howToServe: body.howToServe?.trim() || null,
      howToServeAr: body.howToServeAr?.trim() || null,
      prepTime: Number(body.prepTime) || 0,
      cookTime: Number(body.cookTime) || 0,
      servings: Number(body.servings) || 1,
      dietaryTags: JSON.stringify(body.dietaryTags ?? []),
      imageUrl: body.imageUrl ?? null,
      videoUrl: body.videoUrl ?? null,
    }).where(eq(recipes.id, recipeId));

    await db.delete(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, recipeId));
    await db.delete(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, recipeId));

    if (body.ingredients?.length) {
      await db.insert(ingredients).values(
        body.ingredients.filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim()).map((i) => ({
          recipeId,
          name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
          nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
          amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
          unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
          unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
          preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
          preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    if (body.instructions?.length) {
      await db.insert(instructions).values(
        body.instructions.filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim()).map((s, idx) => ({
          recipeId, stepNumber: idx + 1,
          text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
          textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ id: recipeId });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Update recipe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to update recipe" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });
    await db.delete(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, recipeId));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Delete recipe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to delete recipe" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/bulk/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";

type BulkRecipe = {
  name: string;
  nameAr?: string;
  description?: string;
  descriptionAr?: string;
  cuisine?: string;
  cuisineAr?: string;
  prepTime?: number;
  cookTime?: number;
  servings?: number;
  dietaryTags?: string[];
  imageUrl?: string | null;
  videoUrl?: string | null;
  ingredients?: {
    name: string;
    nameAr?: string;
    amount?: string;
    unit?: string;
    unitAr?: string;
    preparation?: string;
    preparationAr?: string;
  }[];
  instructions?: { text: string; textAr?: string }[];
};

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const list: BulkRecipe[] = Array.isArray(body) ? body : body.recipes;

    if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Expected non-empty array" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const ids: number[] = [];

    for (const r of list) {
      if (!r.name?.trim() && !r.nameAr?.trim()) continue;

      const [inserted] = await db
        .insert(recipes)
        .values({
          name: r.name?.trim() || r.nameAr?.trim() || "",
          nameAr: r.nameAr?.trim() || null,
          description: r.description?.trim() || null,
          descriptionAr: r.descriptionAr?.trim() || null,
          cuisine: r.cuisine?.trim() || null,
          cuisineAr: r.cuisineAr?.trim() || null,
          prepTime: Number(r.prepTime) || 0,
          cookTime: Number(r.cookTime) || 0,
          servings: Number(r.servings) || 4,
          dietaryTags: JSON.stringify(r.dietaryTags ?? []),
          imageUrl: r.imageUrl ?? null,
          videoUrl: r.videoUrl ?? null,
        })
        .returning();

      if (r.ingredients?.length) {
        await db.insert(ingredients).values(
          r.ingredients
            .filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim())
            .map((i) => ({
              recipeId: inserted.id,
              name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
              nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
              amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
              unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
              unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
              preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
              preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }

      if (r.instructions?.length) {
        await db.insert(instructions).values(
          r.instructions
            .filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim())
            .map((s, idx) => ({
              recipeId: inserted.id,
              stepNumber: idx + 1,
              text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
              textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }

      ids.push(inserted.id);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ inserted: ids.length, ids });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Bulk import error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Bulk import failed: " + String(err) },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/recipes/bulk/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";

// --- ADDED GET ROUTE FOR WHATSAPP SENDER ---
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const all = await db.select().from(recipes).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt));
    return NextResponse.json(all);
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// --- EXISTING POST ROUTE ---
type Payload = {
  name: string; nameAr: string;
  description: string; descriptionAr: string;
  cuisine: string; cuisineAr: string;
  category: string; categoryAr: string;
  servedWith: string; servedWithAr: string;
  howToServe: string; howToServeAr: string;
  prepTime: number; cookTime: number; servings: number;
  dietaryTags: string[]; imageUrl: string | null; videoUrl: string | null;
  ingredients: { name: string; nameAr: string; amount: string; unit: string; unitAr: string; preparation: string; preparationAr: string }[];
  instructions: { text: string; textAr: string }[];
};

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const body: Payload = await req.json();
    if (!body.name?.trim() && !body.nameAr?.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe name is required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const [recipe] = await db.insert(recipes).values({
      name: body.name?.trim() || body.nameAr?.trim() || "",
      nameAr: body.nameAr?.trim() || null,
      description: body.description?.trim() || null,
      descriptionAr: body.descriptionAr?.trim() || null,
      cuisine: body.cuisine?.trim() || null,
      cuisineAr: body.cuisineAr?.trim() || null,
      category: body.category?.trim() || null,
      categoryAr: body.categoryAr?.trim() || null,
      servedWith: body.servedWith?.trim() || null,
      servedWithAr: body.servedWithAr?.trim() || null,
      howToServe: body.howToServe?.trim() || null,
      howToServeAr: body.howToServeAr?.trim() || null,
      prepTime: Number(body.prepTime) || 0,
      cookTime: Number(body.cookTime) || 0,
      servings: Number(body.servings) || 1,
      dietaryTags: JSON.stringify(body.dietaryTags ?? []),
      imageUrl: body.imageUrl ?? null,
      videoUrl: body.videoUrl ?? null,
    }).returning();

    if (body.ingredients?.length) {
      await db.insert(ingredients).values(
        body.ingredients.filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim()).map((i) => ({
          recipeId: recipe.id,
          name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
          nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
          amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
          unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
          unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
          preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
          preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    if (body.instructions?.length) {
      await db.insert(instructions).values(
        body.instructions.filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim()).map((s, idx) => ({
          recipeId: recipe.id, stepNumber: idx + 1,
          text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
          textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ id: recipe.id });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Save recipe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save recipe" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/settings/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { settings } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(settings);
    const data = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));
    return NextResponse.json(data);
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json(); // Expecting { today_color: "#ff0000", ... }

    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(body)) {
      await db.insert(settings)
        .values({ key, value: String(value) })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: settings.key, set: { value: String(value) } });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/settings/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/share/whatsapp/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc } from "drizzle-orm";

const BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://food-menu-app-xet2.onrender.com";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const id = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id");
  const lang = (req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("lang") || "en") as "en" | "ar";

  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "id required" }, { status: 400 });

  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid id" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });

  const ings = await db
    .select()
    .from(ingredients)
    .where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, numericId))
    .orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));

  const steps = await db
    .select()
    .from(instructions)
    .where(eq(instructions.recipeId, numericId))
    .orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));

  const totalTime = recipe.prepTime + recipe.cookTime;
  const link = `${BASE_URL}/recipes/${recipe.id}`;

  const isAr = lang === "ar";

  const name = isAr ? (recipe.nameAr || recipe.name) : recipe.name;
  const desc = isAr ? (recipe.descriptionAr || recipe.description || "") : (recipe.description || "");
  const cuisine = isAr ? (recipe.cuisineAr || recipe.cuisine || "") : (recipe.cuisine || "");
  const servedWith = isAr ? (recipe.servedWithAr || recipe.servedWith || "") : (recipe.servedWith || "");
  const howToServe = isAr ? (recipe.howToServeAr || recipe.howToServe || "") : (recipe.howToServe || "");

  const topIngs = ings
    .slice(0, 4)
    .map((i) => (isAr ? (i.nameAr || i.name) : i.name))
    .filter(Boolean);

  const firstStep = steps[0]
    ? (isAr ? (steps[0].textAr || steps[0].text) : steps[0].text)
    : "";

  const message = isAr
    ? buildArabic({ name, desc, cuisine, totalTime, servings: recipe.servings, topIngs, firstStep, servedWith, howToServe, link })
    : buildEnglish({ name, desc, cuisine, totalTime, servings: recipe.servings, topIngs, firstStep, servedWith, howToServe, link });

  const encoded = encodeURIComponent(message);
  const url = `https://wa.me/?text=${encoded}`;

  return NextResponse.json({ message, url });
}

function buildEnglish(d: {
  name: string; desc: string; cuisine: string; totalTime: number;
  servings: number; topIngs: string[]; firstStep: string;
  servedWith: string; howToServe: string; link: string;
}) {
  const ingLine = d.topIngs.length ? d.topIngs.join(" • ") : "";
  return `╭─────────────────────╮
   ✦  NEW RECIPE  ✦
╰─────────────────────╯

🍽️ *${d.name}*
_${[d.cuisine, `${d.totalTime} min`, `serves ${d.servings}`].filter(Boolean).join(" • ")}_

─────────────────────

${d.desc ? `_${d.desc}_\n\n` : ""}🛒 *You'll need*
${ingLine}

${d.firstStep ? `👨‍🍳 *How it starts*\n${d.firstStep}\n\n` : ""}─────────────────────
${d.servedWith ? `\n🍽️ *Serve with*\n${d.servedWith}\n` : ""}${d.howToServe ? `\n📌 *Tip*\n${d.howToServe}\n` : ""}
─────────────────────

👉 Full recipe with photos & steps:
${d.link}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
🕌 _Menu — your cookbook_`;
}

function buildArabic(d: {
  name: string; desc: string; cuisine: string; totalTime: number;
  servings: number; topIngs: string[]; firstStep: string;
  servedWith: string; howToServe: string; link: string;
}) {
  const ingLine = d.topIngs.length ? d.topIngs.join(" • ") : "";
  return `╭─────────────────────╮
   ✦  وصفة جديدة  ✦
╰─────────────────────╯

🍽️ *${d.name}*
_${[d.cuisine, `${d.totalTime} دقيقة`, `يكفي ${d.servings}`].filter(Boolean).join(" • ")}_

─────────────────────

${d.desc ? `_${d.desc}_\n\n` : ""}🛒 *المكونات الأساسية*
${ingLine}

${d.firstStep ? `👨‍🍳 *الطريقة*\n${d.firstStep}\n\n` : ""}─────────────────────
${d.servedWith ? `\n🍽️ *يقدم مع*\n${d.servedWith}\n` : ""}${d.howToServe ? `\n📌 *طريقة التقديم*\n${d.howToServe}\n` : ""}
─────────────────────

👉 الوصفة كاملة بالصور:
${d.link}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
🕌 _منيو — كتاب وصفاتك_`;
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/share/whatsapp/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/upload/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { PutObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";
import { r2, R2_BUCKET } from "@/lib/r2";
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";

const ALLOWED_TYPES = [
  "image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif",
  "video/mp4", "video/webm", "video/quicktime",
];

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { filename, contentType } = await req.json();

    if (!filename || !contentType) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "filename and contentType are required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.includes(contentType)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unsupported file type" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const ext = filename.split(".").pop() ?? "bin";
    const isVideo = contentType.startsWith("video/");
    const folder = isVideo ? "videos" : "recipes";
    const key = `${folder}/${randomUUID()}.${ext}`;

    const command = new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: R2_BUCKET,
      Key: key,
      ContentType: contentType,
    });

    const uploadUrl = await getSignedUrl(r2, command, {
      expiresIn: isVideo ? 900 : 60,
      unhoistableHeaders: new Set(["x-amz-checksum-crc32"]),
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ uploadUrl, key, isVideo });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Upload presign error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to create upload URL" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/upload/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/inbox/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type Notification = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  url: string | null;
  previewImage: string | null;
  previewTitle: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
};

export default function InboxPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Notification[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  
  const [colors, setColors] = useState({
    inbox_title_color_light: "#e2543a",
    inbox_title_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
    inbox_recipe_color_light: "#52525b",
    inbox_recipe_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me").then((res) => res.json()).then((data) => setIsAdmin(!!data.admin)).catch(() => setIsAdmin(false));
    fetch("/api/settings").then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
      if (data.inbox_title_color_light) setColors(prev => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
    });
    loadMessages();
  }, []);

  function loadMessages() {
    fetch("/api/notifications")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  async function handleDelete(id: number) {
    const confirmDelete = window.confirm(lang === "ar" ? "حذف الرسالة؟" : "Delete message?");
    if (!confirmDelete) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notifications/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed");
      setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    } catch (err) {
      alert("Delete failed");
    }
  }

  function renderPreviewTitle(title: string) {
    // Check if title contains the hyphen split (e.g., "Hicham's... - Musakhan")
    if (title.includes(" - ")) {
      const [mainTitle, recipeName] = title.split(" - ");
      return (
        <span style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
          <span 
            className="custom-color-text"
            style={{ "--custom-light": colors.inbox_title_color_light, "--custom-dark": colors.inbox_title_color_dark, fontWeight: 700 } as React.CSSProperties}
          >
            {mainTitle}
          </span>
          <span 
            className="custom-color-text"
            style={{ "--custom-light": colors.inbox_recipe_color_light, "--custom-dark": colors.inbox_recipe_color_dark, fontWeight: 500, fontSize: 12 } as React.CSSProperties}
          >
            {recipeName}
          </span>
        </span>
      );
    }
    return <strong style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink)" }}>{title}</strong>;
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "صندوق الإشعارات" : "Inbox"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">{lang === "ar" ? "كل التحديثات السابقة" : "All previous updates"}</p>

      {loading ? (
        <p className="helper">{lang === "ar" ? "جاري التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</p>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{lang === "ar" ? "لا توجد إشعارات" : "No notifications yet"}</h2>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16, marginTop: 24 }}>
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", padding: 16, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 16 }}>
                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)" }}>{item.title}</h3>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                    {new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                  </span>
                  {isAdmin && (
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(item.id)} style={{ background: "transparent", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 14 }}>🗑</button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{item.body}</p>

              {/* Show Image Preview Card (if it exists) */}
              {item.url && item.previewImage ? (
                <Link href={item.url} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, padding: 10, background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, textDecoration: "none", color: "inherit", marginTop: 4 }}>
                  <img src={item.previewImage} alt="Preview" style={{ width: 60, height: 60, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6 }} />
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
                    {item.previewTitle ? renderPreviewTitle(item.previewTitle) : <strong>View Recipe</strong>}
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)", marginTop: 2 }}>{item.url.replace("https://", "")}</span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ) : (
                /* Only show the button if there is NO image preview */
                item.url && item.url !== "/inbox" && (
                  <Link href={item.url} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ alignSelf: "flex-start", marginTop: 4 }}>
                    {lang === "ar" ? "عرض الصفحة" : "View Page"} →
                  </Link>
                )
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/inbox/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/layout.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import "./globals.css";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/ThemeProvider";
import Nav from "@/components/Nav";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Menu — Recipes",
  description: "Your personal cookbook",
};

// Runs before React hydration — applies saved theme/lang instantly.
// No flash of dark/English on first paint.
const inlineBootstrap = `
(function() {
  try {
    var theme = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    if (!theme) {
      theme = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches ? "light" : "dark";
    }
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

    var lang = localStorage.getItem("lang") || "en";
    document.documentElement.lang = lang;
    document.documentElement.dir = lang === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
  } catch(e) {}
})();
`;

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" data-theme="dark" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <script dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: inlineBootstrap }} />
      </head>
      <body>
        <ThemeProvider>
          <LanguageProvider>
            <Nav />
            {children}
          </LanguageProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/layout.tsx ---

            <div className="recipe-card-img" style={{ background: "#e8e2d8" }} />
            <div className="recipe-card-body">
              <div style={{ height: 18, background: "#e8e2d8", borderRadius: 4, marginBottom: 8 }} />
              <div style={{ height: 14, background: "#e8e2d8", borderRadius: 4, width: "60%" }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}--- END FILE: src/app/loading.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/login/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(data.error || "Login failed");
      }
      window.location.href = "/";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Login failed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="login-card">
      <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "تسجيل الدخول" : "Sign in"}</h1>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "اسم المستخدم" : "Username"}</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={username}
            onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
            required
          />
        </div>
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "كلمة المرور" : "Password"}</label>
          <input
            type="password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 12, margin: "0 0 12px" }}>{error}</p>
        )}
        <button
          type="submit"
          className="btn btn-primary"
          disabled={busy}
          style={{ width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", padding: "10px 16px" }}
        >
          {busy ? "…" : lang === "ar" ? "دخول" : "Continue"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/login/page.tsx ---

// src/app/page.tsx
import { connection } from "next/server";

import { recipes } from "@/db/schema";

import HomeClient from "@/components/HomeClient";

async function RecipeList() {
  await connection();
  const db = getDb();
  const all = await db.select().from(recipes).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt));
  return <HomeClient recipes={all} />;
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/edit/page.tsx ---
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc } from "drizzle-orm";
import RecipeForm from "@/components/RecipeForm";
import EditRecipeShell from "@/components/EditRecipeShell";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

async function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) redirect("/login");
  return <>{children}</>;
}

async function EditLoader({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) notFound();

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) notFound();

  const ings = await db.select().from(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, numericId)).orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));
  const steps = await db.select().from(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, numericId)).orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));

  return (
    <RecipeForm
      initial={{
        id: recipe.id,
        name: recipe.name,
        nameAr: recipe.nameAr,
        description: recipe.description,
        descriptionAr: recipe.descriptionAr,
        cuisine: recipe.cuisine,
        cuisineAr: recipe.cuisineAr,
        category: recipe.category,
        categoryAr: recipe.categoryAr,
        servedWith: recipe.servedWith,
        servedWithAr: recipe.servedWithAr,
        howToServe: recipe.howToServe,
        howToServeAr: recipe.howToServeAr,
        prepTime: recipe.prepTime,
        cookTime: recipe.cookTime,
        servings: recipe.servings,
        dietaryTags: recipe.dietaryTags,
        imageUrl: recipe.imageUrl,
        videoUrl: recipe.videoUrl,
        ingredients: ings.map((i) => ({
          name: i.name, nameAr: i.nameAr, amount: i.amount, unit: i.unit,
          unitAr: i.unitAr, preparation: i.preparation, preparationAr: i.preparationAr,
        })),
        instructions: steps.map((s) => ({ text: s.text, textAr: s.textAr })),
      }}
    />
  );
}

function FormSkeleton() {
  return <div className="form-card" style={{ height: 400, opacity: 0.4 }} />;
}

export default function EditRecipePage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <EditRecipeShell>
        <Suspense fallback={<FormSkeleton />}>
          <AuthGate>
            <EditLoader params={params} />
          </AuthGate>
        </Suspense>
      </EditRecipeShell>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/page.tsx ---
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions, settings } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc } from "drizzle-orm";
import RecipeDetailClient from "@/components/RecipeDetailClient";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import type { Metadata } from "next";

export async function generateMetadata({ 
  params, searchParams 
}: { 
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const search = await searchParams;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) return { title: "Recipe Not Found" };

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) return { title: "Recipe Not Found" };

  const settingsRows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(settingsRows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));

  const isToday = "today" in search;
  const customTitle = s.today_text || "Hicham's Main Dish of the day";
  
  const title = isToday ? `${customTitle} - ${recipe.name}` : recipe.name;
  const description = recipe.description || "View this recipe on Menu.";
  const image = recipe.imageUrl || "https://food-menu-app-xxww.onrender.com/icon-192.png";

  return {
    title,
    description,
    openGraph: { title, description, images: [{ url: image }], type: "article" },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title, description, images: [image] },
  };
}

async function RecipeDetail({ id, isToday }: { id: number; isToday: boolean; }) {
  const db = getDb();
  
  const settingsRows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(settingsRows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));

  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) notFound();

  const customTitle = s.today_text || "Hicham's Main Dish of the day";

  const displayRecipe = isToday 
    ? { 
        ...recipe, 
        name: (
          <>
            <span 
              className="custom-color-text"
              style={{ 
                "--custom-light": s.today_color_light || "#e2543a",
                "--custom-dark": s.today_color_dark || "#ff6b4a"
              } as React.CSSProperties}
            >
              {customTitle}
            </span>
            <br />
            <span 
              className="custom-color-text"
              style={{ 
                "--custom-light": s.recipe_name_color_light || "#52525b",
                "--custom-dark": s.recipe_name_color_dark || "#a1a1aa",
                fontSize: "0.75em", 
                fontWeight: 400, 
                display: "block", 
                marginTop: 8 
              } as React.CSSProperties}
            >
              {recipe.name}
            </span>
          </>
        ) 
      } 
    : recipe;

  const ings = await db.select().from(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, id)).orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));
  const steps = await db.select().from(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, id)).orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));
  const admin = await isAdmin();

  return (
    <RecipeDetailClient
      recipe={displayRecipe as any}
      ingredients={ings}
      instructions={steps}
      isAdmin={admin}
    />
  );
}

function DetailSkeleton() {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="detail-hero" style={{ background: "#e8e2d8", opacity: 0.5 }} />
      <div style={{ height: 40, background: "#e8e2d8", margin: "24px 0", borderRadius: 8, width: "60%" }} />
    </>
  );
}

async function RecipeResolver({ params, searchParams }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }>; searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>; }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const search = await searchParams;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) notFound();
  return <RecipeDetail id={numericId} isToday={"today" in search} />;
}

export default function RecipePage({ params, searchParams }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }>; searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>; }) {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <Suspense fallback={<DetailSkeleton />}>
        <RecipeResolver params={params} searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/settings/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();
  
  const [config, setConfig] = useState({
    today_text: "Hicham's Main Dish of the day",
    today_color_light: "#e2543a",
    today_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
    recipe_name_color_light: "#52525b",
    recipe_name_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
    inbox_title_color_light: "#e2543a",
    inbox_title_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
    inbox_recipe_color_light: "#52525b",
    inbox_recipe_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
  });
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else loadSettings();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function loadSettings() {
    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaving(true);
    await fetch("/api/settings", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(config),
    });
    setSaving(false);
    alert(lang === "ar" ? "تم الحفظ!" : "Settings saved!");
  }

  const ColorPair = ({ label, lightKey, darkKey }: { label: string, lightKey: keyof typeof config, darkKey: keyof typeof config }) => (
    <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
      <label>{label}</label>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>Light Mode</span>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <input type="color" value={config[lightKey] as string} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [lightKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ width: 40, height: 36, padding: 0, border: "none", background: "none" }} />
            <input type="text" value={config[lightKey] as string} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [lightKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>Dark Mode</span>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <input type="color" value={config[darkKey] as string} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [darkKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ width: 40, height: 36, padding: 0, border: "none", background: "none" }} />
            <input type="text" value={config[darkKey] as string} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [darkKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>Loading...</div>;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 650 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "إعدادات العناوين" : "Title & Color Settings"}</h1>
      </div>
      
      <div className="form-card">
        {/* Text Customization */}
        <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <label style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
            "Today" Title Text
          </label>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            value={config.today_text} 
            onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, today_text: e.target.value })} 
            placeholder="e.g. Hicham's Main Dish of the day"
            style={{ width: "100%", padding: "10px", marginTop: 8 }}
          />
        </div>

        <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "24px 0" }} />

        <ColorPair label="Main Title Color (?today)" lightKey="today_color_light" darkKey="today_color_dark" />
        <ColorPair label="Recipe Name Color (under ?today)" lightKey="recipe_name_color_light" darkKey="recipe_name_color_dark" />

        <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "24px 0" }} />

        <ColorPair label="Inbox Preview: Main Title Color" lightKey="inbox_title_color_light" darkKey="inbox_title_color_dark" />
        <ColorPair label="Inbox Preview: Recipe Name Color" lightKey="inbox_recipe_color_light" darkKey="inbox_recipe_color_dark" />

        <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>
          {saving ? "Saving..." : (lang === "ar" ? "حفظ التغييرات" : "Save Changes")}
        </button>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/AdminSender.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";

type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
};

export default function AdminSender() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<string>("");
  
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("Menu");
  const [body, setBody] = useState("New recipe added!");
  const [url, setUrl] = useState(""); // Empty by default

  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [showDropdown, setShowDropdown] = useState(false);

  const baseUrl = "https://food-menu-app-xxww.onrender.com";

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/recipes")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => setRecipes(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []))
      .catch(() => setRecipes([]));
  }, []);

  const filteredRecipes = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return recipes;
    const lower = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return recipes.filter(r => 
      r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || 
      (r.nameAr && r.nameAr.includes(searchQuery))
    );
  }, [recipes, searchQuery]);

  function selectRecipe(id: number) {
    setUrl(`/recipes/${id}?today`);
    setSearchQuery("");
    setShowDropdown(false);
  }

  async function send() {
    setBusy(true);
    setResult("");
    try {
      const fullUrl = url.trim().startsWith("/") ? `${baseUrl}${url}` : url.trim();

      const res = await fetch("/api/push/send", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ title, body, url: fullUrl }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setResult(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
    } catch (err) {
      setResult(String(err));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const previewRecipe = useMemo(() => {
    const match = url.match(/\/recipes\/(\d+)/);
    if (match) return recipes.find(r => r.id === parseInt(match[1]));
    return null;
  }, [url, recipes]);

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
      <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "إرسال إشعار" : "Send Push Notification"}</h1>
      
      <div className="form-card">
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "العنوان" : "Title"}</label>
          <input type="text" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        </div>
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "النص" : "Body"}</label>
          <input type="text" value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        </div>

        <div className="field" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "ابحث عن وصفة لربطها (اختياري)" : "Search Recipe (Optional)"}</label>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Type recipe name..." 
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => { setSearchQuery(e.target.value); setShowDropdown(true); }}
            onFocus={() => setShowDropdown(true)}
            style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
          />
          
          {showDropdown && searchQuery.trim() !== "" && (
            <>
              <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 10 }} onClick={() => setShowDropdown(false)} />
              <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: "100%", left: 0, right: 0, maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", background: "var(--surface-solid)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, zIndex: 20, marginTop: 4, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)" }}>
                {filteredRecipes.length === 0 ? (
                  <div style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>No recipes found</div>
                ) : (
                  filteredRecipes.map((r) => (
                    <div key={r.id} onClick={() => selectRecipe(r.id)} style={{ padding: "10px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", fontSize: 14, color: "var(--ink)" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"} onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
                      {lang === "ar" && r.nameAr ? r.nameAr : r.name}
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "الرابط (اختياري)" : "URL (Optional)"}</label>
          <input type="text" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="Leave empty to send text only" style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        </div>

        {url.trim() !== "" && (
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
            <label style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)" }}>Inbox Preview</label>
            <div style={{ background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", padding: 16 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600 }}>{title}</h3>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>Just now</span>
              </div>
              <p style={{ margin: "0 0 12px 0", fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{body}</p>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, padding: 10, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8 }}>
                {previewRecipe?.imageUrl ? <img src={previewRecipe.imageUrl} alt="Preview" style={{ width: 60, height: 60, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6 }} /> : <div style={{ width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 6, background: "var(--line)" }} />}
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{previewRecipe ? previewRecipe.name : "View Link"}</strong>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>{url}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        
        <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={send} disabled={busy} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 12 }}>
          {busy ? "Sending..." : "Send"}
        </button>

        {result && <pre style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 13, background: "#f5f5f5", padding: 12, borderRadius: 8 }}>{result}</pre>}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/AdminSender.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/AutoTranslate.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CUISINES, UNITS, PREPARATIONS, PickerItem } from "@/lib/pickers";

type Kind = "cuisine" | "unit" | "prep";

type Props = {
  kind: Kind;
  value: string;         // current value in the source field
  sourceIsAr?: boolean;  // if true, we're translating AR -> EN
  onTranslated: (result: string) => void;
  title?: string;
};

function findList(kind: Kind): PickerItem[] {
  if (kind === "cuisine") return CUISINES;
  if (kind === "unit") return UNITS;
  return PREPARATIONS;
}

export default function AutoTranslate({ kind, value, sourceIsAr, onTranslated, title }: Props) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  function translate() {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;

    setBusy(true);
    const list = findList(kind);

    // Match against EN value, AR value, or label (case-insensitive)
    const found = list.find((item) => {
      const targets = sourceIsAr
        ? [item.valueAr, item.value, item.label]
        : [item.value, item.label, item.valueAr];
      return targets.some((t) => t.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase());
    });

    if (found) {
      const result = sourceIsAr ? found.value : found.valueAr;
      onTranslated(result);
      setDone(true);
      setTimeout(() => setDone(false), 1200);
    } else {
      // No exact match — try partial startsWith
      const partial = list.find((item) => {
        const target = sourceIsAr ? item.valueAr : item.value;
        return target.toLowerCase().startsWith(trimmed.toLowerCase());
      });
      if (partial) {
        const result = sourceIsAr ? partial.value : partial.valueAr;
        onTranslated(result);
        setDone(true);
        setTimeout(() => setDone(false), 1200);
      }
    }
    setBusy(false);
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={`auto-translate-btn${done ? " done" : ""}`}
      onClick={translate}
      disabled={busy || !value.trim()}
      title={title || "Auto-translate"}
      aria-label="Auto-translate"
    >
      {done ? "✓" : "⇄"}
    </button>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/AutoTranslate.tsx ---
