--- FILE: src/components/HomeClient.tsx ---
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { t, pick } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { cuisineFlagUrl } from "@/lib/cuisine-flag";
import { CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/categories";

type RecipeRow = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  cuisine: string | null;
  cuisineAr: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
  prepTime: number;
  cookTime: number;
  servings: number;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  videoUrl: string | null;
  dietaryTags: string | null;
};

const TAG_AR: Record<string, string> = {
  Vegetarian: "نباتي", Vegan: "نباتي صرف", Pescatarian: "بيسكاتاري",
  "Gluten-Free": "خالٍ من الغلوتين", "Dairy-Free": "خالٍ من الألبان",
  "Nut-Free": "خالٍ من المكسرات", Halal: "حلال", Kosher: "كوشير",
  "Low-Carb": "قليل الكربوهيدرات", Keto: "كيتو", Paleo: "باليو",
  Meat: "لحوم", Chicken: "دجاج", Seafood: "مأكولات بحرية",
  Fish: "سمك", Lamb: "لحم غنم", Beef: "لحم بقري",
  Legumes: "بقوليات", Spicy: "حار", Quick: "سريع",
};

type SortKey = "newest" | "name-asc" | "name-desc" | "time-asc" | "time-desc";

export default function HomeClient({ recipes }: { recipes: RecipeRow[] }) {
  const { lang } = useLang();

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [cuisineFilter, setCuisineFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [tagFilter, setTagFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortKey>("newest");

  const allCuisines = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of recipes) {
      if (r.cuisine) set.add(r.cuisine);
      if (r.cuisineAr && !r.cuisine) set.add(r.cuisineAr);
    }
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [recipes]);

  const allTags = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of recipes) {
      if (!r.dietaryTags) continue;
      try {
        const tags = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
        if (Array.isArray(tags)) tags.forEach((tag) => set.add(tag));
      } catch {}
    }
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [recipes]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();

    let list = recipes.filter((r) => {
      const name = (pick(lang, r.name, r.nameAr) || "").toLowerCase();
      const cuisine = (pick(lang, r.cuisine, r.cuisineAr) || "").toLowerCase();

      if (q && !name.includes(q) && !cuisine.includes(q)) return false;
      if (cuisineFilter && (r.cuisine || r.cuisineAr) !== cuisineFilter) return false;
      if (categoryFilter && (r.category || r.categoryAr) !== categoryFilter) return false;

      if (tagFilter) {
        if (!r.dietaryTags) return false;
        try {
          const tags = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
          if (!Array.isArray(tags) || !tags.includes(tagFilter)) return false;
        } catch {
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    });

    list = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === "name-asc") return (pick(lang, a.name, a.nameAr) || "").localeCompare(pick(lang, b.name, b.nameAr) || "");
      if (sort === "name-desc") return (pick(lang, b.name, b.nameAr) || "").localeCompare(pick(lang, a.name, a.nameAr) || "");
      if (sort === "time-asc") return a.prepTime + a.cookTime - (b.prepTime + b.cookTime);
      if (sort === "time-desc") return b.prepTime + b.cookTime - (a.prepTime + a.cookTime);
      return 0;
    });

    return list;
  }, [recipes, query, cuisineFilter, categoryFilter, tagFilter, sort, lang]);

  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const groups: { key: string; icon: string; label: string; items: RecipeRow[] }[] = [];

    for (const c of CATEGORIES) {
      const items = filtered.filter(
        (r) =>
          (r.category || "") === c.value ||
          (r.categoryAr || "") === c.valueAr ||
          (r.category || "") === c.valueAr ||
          (r.categoryAr || "") === c.value
      );
      if (items.length > 0) {
        groups.push({
          key: c.value,
          icon: c.icon,
          label: lang === "ar" ? c.valueAr : c.value,
          items,
        });
      }
    }

    const uncategorized = filtered.filter((r) => {
      const cat = (r.category || "").trim();
      const catAr = (r.categoryAr || "").trim();
      if (!cat && !catAr) return true;
      return !CATEGORIES.some(
        (c) =>
          c.value === cat ||
          c.valueAr === cat ||
          c.value === catAr ||
          c.valueAr === catAr
      );
    });

    if (uncategorized.length > 0) {
      groups.push({
        key: "__other__",
        icon: "🍴",
        label: lang === "ar" ? "أخرى" : "Other",
        items: uncategorized,
      });
    }

    return groups;
  }, [filtered, lang]);

  const hasFilters = query || cuisineFilter || categoryFilter || tagFilter;
  const total = recipes.length;

  function clearFilters() {
    setQuery("");
    setCuisineFilter("");
    setCategoryFilter("");
    setTagFilter("");
    setSort("newest");
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{t(lang, "home_title")}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {total === 0
          ? ""
          : hasFilters
          ? lang === "ar"
            ? `${filtered.length} من ${total} وصفة`
            : `${filtered.length} of ${total} recipes`
          : total === 1
          ? t(lang, "recipe_count_one")
          : t(lang, "recipe_count").replace("{n}", String(total))}
      </p>

      {total > 0 && (
        <div className="filter-bar">
          <div className="search-wrap">
            <span className="search-icon">🔍</span>
            <input
              type="text"
              className="search-input"
              placeholder={lang === "ar" ? "ابحث عن وصفة..." : "Search recipes..."}
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            />
            {query && (
              <button type="button" className="search-clear" onClick={() => setQuery("")} aria-label="Clear search">✕</button>
            )}
          </div>

          <select className="filter-select" value={categoryFilter} onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">{lang === "ar" ? "كل الفئات" : "All categories"}</option>
            {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>{c.icon} {lang === "ar" ? c.valueAr : c.value}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select className="filter-select" value={cuisineFilter} onChange={(e) => setCuisineFilter(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">{lang === "ar" ? "كل المطابخ" : "All cuisines"}</option>
            {allCuisines.map((c) => (
              <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select className="filter-select" value={tagFilter} onChange={(e) => setTagFilter(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">{lang === "ar" ? "كل الوسوم" : "All tags"}</option>
            {allTags.map((tag) => (
              <option key={tag} value={tag}>{lang === "ar" ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select className="filter-select" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortKey)}>
            <option value="newest">{lang === "ar" ? "الأحدث" : "Newest"}</option>
            <option value="name-asc">{lang === "ar" ? "الاسم أ-ي" : "Name A–Z"}</option>
            <option value="name-desc">{lang === "ar" ? "الاسم ي-أ" : "Name Z–A"}</option>
            <option value="time-asc">{lang === "ar" ? "الأسرع أولاً" : "Fastest first"}</option>
            <option value="time-desc">{lang === "ar" ? "الأطول أولاً" : "Longest first"}</option>
          </select>

          {hasFilters && (
            <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={clearFilters}>{lang === "ar" ? "مسح" : "Clear"}</button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {total === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{t(lang, "home_empty_title")}</h2>
          <p>{t(lang, "home_empty_text")}</p>
          <Link href="/recipes/new" className="btn btn-primary">{t(lang, "home_empty_cta")}</Link>
        </div>
      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{lang === "ar" ? "لا نتائج" : "No results"}</h2>
          <p>{lang === "ar" ? "جرّب كلمات أخرى أو امسح الفلاتر." : "Try different keywords or clear your filters."}</p>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={clearFilters}>{lang === "ar" ? "مسح الفلاتر" : "Clear filters"}</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="category-sections">
          {grouped.map((group) => (
            <section key={group.key} className="category-section">
              <div className="category-section-head">
                <h2 className="category-section-title">
                  <span className="category-section-icon">{group.icon}</span>
                  <span>{group.label}</span>
                </h2>
                <span className="category-section-count">{group.items.length}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="category-section-line" />
              <div className="recipe-grid">
                {group.items.map((r) => {
                  const tags: string[] = r.dietaryTags ? JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags) : [];
                  const displayName = pick(lang, r.name, r.nameAr);
                  const displayCuisine = pick(lang, r.cuisine, r.cuisineAr);
                  const flagUrl = cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisine) || cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisineAr);
                  const hasVideo = !!(r.videoUrl && r.videoUrl.trim());

                  return (
                    <Link key={r.id} href={`/recipes/${r.id}`} className="recipe-card">
                      <div className="recipe-card-img-wrap">
                        {r.imageUrl ? (
                          <img src={r.imageUrl} alt={displayName} className="recipe-card-img" />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="recipe-card-img" />
                        )}
                        {hasVideo && (
                          <span
                            className="recipe-card-video-emoji"
                            title={lang === "ar" ? "يحتوي على فيديو" : "Has video"}
                          >
                            🎬
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div className="recipe-card-body">
                        <div className="recipe-card-head">
                          <div className="recipe-card-text">
                            <h3>{displayName}</h3>
                            {displayCuisine && (
                              <span className="recipe-card-cuisine">{displayCuisine}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          {flagUrl && (
                            <img src={flagUrl} alt={displayCuisine} className="recipe-card-flag" loading="lazy" />
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="recipe-card-meta">
                          <span>⏱ {r.prepTime + r.cookTime} {lang === "ar" ? "د" : "min"}</span>
                          <span>🍽 {r.servings}</span>
                        </div>

                        {tags.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="recipe-card-tags">
                            {tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => (
                              <span key={tag} className="chip">
                                {lang === "ar" ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}
                              </span>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/ImageUpload.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  onUploaded: (publicUrl: string) => void;
  initialUrl?: string;
};

export default function ImageUpload({ onUploaded, initialUrl }: Props) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(initialUrl ?? null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleFile(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setError(null);
    setUploading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/upload", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ filename: file.name, contentType: file.type }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to get upload URL");
      const { uploadUrl, key } = await res.json();

      const put = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
        method: "PUT",
        body: file,
        headers: { "Content-Type": file.type },
      });

      if (!put.ok) throw new Error("Upload to R2 failed");

      const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_R2_PUBLIC_URL;
      const publicUrl = `${base}/${key}`;

      setPreview(publicUrl);
      onUploaded(publicUrl);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <input
        type="file"
        accept="image/*"
        onChange={handleFile}
        disabled={uploading}
        className="block w-full text-sm"
      />
      {uploading && <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Uploading…</p>}
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>}
      {preview && (
        <img
          src={preview}
          alt="Recipe preview"
          className="w-48 h-48 object-cover rounded-lg border"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/ImageUpload.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/LanguageProvider.tsx ---
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { Lang } from "@/lib/i18n";

type Ctx = {
  lang: Lang;
  setLang: (l: Lang) => void;
  toggle: () => void;
};

const LanguageContext = createContext<Ctx>({
  lang: "en",
  setLang: () => {},
  toggle: () => {},
});

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Lang>("en");

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem("lang") as Lang | null;
    if (stored === "ar" || stored === "en") setLangState(stored);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = lang;
    document.documentElement.dir = lang === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
    localStorage.setItem("lang", lang);
  }, [lang]);

  function setLang(l: Lang) {
    setLangState(l);
  }

  function toggle() {
    setLangState((prev) => (prev === "en" ? "ar" : "en"));
  }

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, toggle }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useLang() {
  return useContext(LanguageContext);
}
--- END FILE: src/components/LanguageProvider.tsx ---

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { useTheme } from "./ThemeProvider";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import PushSubscribe from "./PushSubscribe";

export default function Nav() {
  const { lang, toggle: toggleLang } = useLang();
  const { theme, toggle: toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => setIsAdmin(!!data.admin))
      .catch(() => setIsAdmin(false))
      .finally(() => setLoaded(true));
  }, []);

  async function logout() {
    await fetch("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
    window.location.href = "/";
  }

  return (
    <nav className="nav">
      <div className="nav-inner">
        <Link href="/" className="brand">
          {t(lang, "brand")}<span>.</span>
        </Link>

        <div className="nav-links">
          <Link href="/recipes" className="nav-link">
            {t(lang, "nav_recipes")}
          </Link>

          {/* EVERYONE CAN SEE THE INBOX */}
          <Link href="/inbox" className="nav-link">
            {lang === "ar" ? "📥 الرسائل" : "📥 Inbox"}
          </Link>

          {/* ONLY ADMIN CAN SEE ADD, PUSH, AND LOGOUT */}
{loaded && isAdmin && (
  <>
    <Link href="/recipes/new" className="nav-cta">
      {t(lang, "nav_add")}
    </Link>
    <Link href="/admin" className="nav-link">
      {lang === "ar" ? "🔔 إشعار" : "🔔 Push"}
    </Link>
    <Link href="/whatsapp" className="nav-link">
      💬 {lang === "ar" ? "واتساب" : "WhatsApp"}
    </Link>
    <Link href="/settings" className="nav-link">
      ⚙️ {lang === "ar" ? "الإعدادات" : "Settings"}
    </Link>
    <button type="button" onClick={logout} className="nav-link nav-button">
      {lang === "ar" ? "خروج" : "Logout"}
    </button>
  </>
)}

          {/* ONLY NON-ADMINS SEE THE LOGIN BUTTON */}
          {loaded && !isAdmin && (
            <Link href="/login" className="nav-link">
              {lang === "ar" ? "دخول" : "Login"}
            </Link>
          )}

          <PushSubscribe />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            className="nav-icon-btn"
            aria-label="Toggle theme"
            title={theme === "dark" ? "Switch to light" : "Switch to dark"}
          >
            {theme === "dark" ? "☀" : "☾"}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleLang}
            className="nav-icon-btn nav-lang"
            aria-label="Toggle language"
          >
            🌐 <span>{t(lang, "lang_toggle")}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/PickerDropdown.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { PickerItem } from "@/lib/pickers";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";

type Props = {
  items: PickerItem[];
  onPick: (en: string, ar: string) => void;
  align?: "left" | "right";
  title?: string;
};

export default function PickerDropdown({ items, onPick, align = "right", title }: Props) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function onClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }
    if (open) document.addEventListener("mousedown", onClick);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onClick);
  }, [open]);

  const filtered = query.trim()
    ? items.filter((i) =>
        (i.label + " " + i.value + " " + i.valueAr).toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
      )
    : items;

  const grouped: Record<string, PickerItem[]> = {};
  for (const item of filtered) {
    const g = item.group || "";
    if (!grouped[g]) grouped[g] = [];
    grouped[g].push(item);
  }

  return (
    <div className="picker-wrap" ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="picker-btn"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        title={title || (lang === "ar" ? "اختر من القائمة" : "Pick from list")}
        aria-label="Open picker"
      >
        ▾
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          className="picker-menu"
          style={{
            [align === "right" ? "right" : "left"]: 0,
          } as React.CSSProperties}
        >
          {items.length > 8 && (
            <input
              className="picker-search"
              placeholder={lang === "ar" ? "ابحث..." : "Search..."}
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              autoFocus
            />
          )}

          <div className="picker-list">
            {Object.entries(grouped).map(([group, list]) => (
              <div key={group}>
                {group && <div className="picker-group">{group}</div>}
                {list.map((item) => (
                  <button
                    key={item.value}
                    type="button"
                    className="picker-item"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onPick(item.value, item.valueAr);
                      setOpen(false);
                      setQuery("");
                    }}
                  >
                    <span className="picker-icon">{item.icon}</span>
                    <span className="picker-label">
                      {lang === "ar" ? item.valueAr : item.label}
                    </span>
                    {item.group && <span className="picker-hint">{item.label}</span>}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ))}
            {filtered.length === 0 && (
              <div className="picker-empty">
                {lang === "ar" ? "لا نتائج" : "No results"}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/PushSubscribe.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";

export default function PushSubscribe() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [supported, setSupported] = useState(false);
  const [subscribed, setSubscribed] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if ("serviceWorker" in navigator && "PushManager" in window) {
      setSupported(true);
      navigator.serviceWorker.ready.then((reg) => {
        reg.pushManager.getSubscription().then((sub) => setSubscribed(!!sub));
      });
    }
  }, []);

  async function subscribe() {
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg("");
    try {
      const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
      if (permission !== "granted") {
        setMsg("Permission denied");
        setBusy(false);
        return;
      }

      const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js");
      await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;

      const sub = await reg.pushManager.subscribe({
        userVisibleOnly: true,
        applicationServerKey: urlBase64ToUint8Array(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY!),
      });

      const res = await fetch("/api/push/subscribe", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ subscription: sub }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error("Server rejected the subscription");
      }

      setSubscribed(true);
      setMsg("Subscribed successfully!");
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function unsubscribe() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
      const sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
      if (sub) {
        await fetch("/api/push/unsubscribe", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ endpoint: sub.endpoint }),
        });
        await sub.unsubscribe();
      }
      setSubscribed(false);
      setMsg("Unsubscribed");
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (!supported) return null;

  if (subscribed) {
    return (
      <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={unsubscribe} disabled={busy}>
        🔕 Disable
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, alignItems: "center" }}>
      <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={subscribe} disabled={busy}>
        🔔 Enable
      </button>
      {msg && <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{msg}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

function urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64String: string) {
  const padding = "=".repeat((4 - (base64String.length % 4)) % 4);
  const base64 = (base64String + padding).replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
  const raw = atob(base64);
  const arr = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) arr[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
  return arr;
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { t, pick } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { extractYouTubeId } from "@/lib/youtube";
import { categoryIcon, categoryLabel } from "@/lib/categories";

type RecipeRow = {
  id: number; name: string; nameAr: string | null;
  description: string | null; descriptionAr: string | null;
  cuisine: string | null; cuisineAr: string | null;
  category: string | null; categoryAr: string | null;
  servedWith: string | null; servedWithAr: string | null;
  howToServe: string | null; howToServeAr: string | null;
  prepTime: number; cookTime: number; servings: number;
  dietaryTags: string | null; imageUrl: string | null; videoUrl: string | null;
};
type IngredientRow = {
  id: number; name: string; nameAr: string | null;
  amount: string | null; unit: string | null; unitAr: string | null;
  preparation: string | null; preparationAr: string | null;
};
type InstructionRow = { id: number; stepNumber: number; text: string; textAr: string | null };

const TAG_AR: Record<string, string> = {
  Vegetarian: "نباتي", Vegan: "نباتي صرف", Pescatarian: "بيسكاتاري",
  "Gluten-Free": "خالٍ من الغلوتين", "Dairy-Free": "خالٍ من الألبان",
  "Nut-Free": "خالٍ من المكسرات", Halal: "حلال", Kosher: "كوشير",
  "Low-Carb": "قليل الكربوهيدرات", Keto: "كيتو", Paleo: "باليو",
  Meat: "لحوم", Chicken: "دجاج", Seafood: "مأكولات بحرية",
  Fish: "سمك", Lamb: "لحم غنم", Beef: "لحم بقري",
  Legumes: "بقوليات", Spicy: "حار", Quick: "سريع",
};

export default function RecipeDetailClient({
  recipe, ingredients, instructions, isAdmin,
}: {
  recipe: RecipeRow;
  ingredients: IngredientRow[];
  instructions: InstructionRow[];
  isAdmin: boolean;
}) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

  const tags: string[] = recipe.dietaryTags ? JSON.parse(recipe.dietaryTags) : [];
  const youtubeId = extractYouTubeId(recipe.videoUrl);
  const minLabel = lang === "ar" ? "د" : "min";

  const displayName = typeof recipe.name === "string" 
  ? pick(lang, recipe.name, recipe.nameAr) 
  : recipe.name;
  const displayDesc = pick(lang, recipe.description, recipe.descriptionAr);
  const displayCuisine = pick(lang, recipe.cuisine, recipe.cuisineAr);
  const displayServedWith = pick(lang, recipe.servedWith, recipe.servedWithAr);
  const displayHowToServe = pick(lang, recipe.howToServe, recipe.howToServeAr);
  const catIcon = categoryIcon(recipe.category) || categoryIcon(recipe.categoryAr);
  const catLabel = categoryLabel(recipe.category, lang) || categoryLabel(recipe.categoryAr, lang);

  function shareToWhatsApp(withToday: boolean) {
    const origin = typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.location.origin : "";
    const url = `${origin}/recipes/${recipe.id}${withToday ? "?today" : ""}`;
    const waUrl = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`;
    window.open(waUrl, "_blank");
  }

  async function handleDelete() {
    const ok = window.confirm(
      lang === "ar"
        ? `هل أنت متأكد من حذف "${displayName}"؟ لا يمكن التراجع.`
        : `Delete "${displayName}"? This cannot be undone.`
    );
    if (!ok) return;
    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/recipes/${recipe.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Delete failed");
      router.push("/");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(lang === "ar" ? "فشل الحذف" : "Delete failed");
      setDeleting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div style={{ margin: "20px 0" }}>
        <Link href="/" className="btn btn-ghost">{t(lang, "back_cookbook")}</Link>
      </div>

      {recipe.imageUrl && (
        <img src={recipe.imageUrl} alt={displayName} className="detail-hero" />
      )}

      <header className="detail-head">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <h1 style={{ margin: 0 }}>{displayName}</h1>

          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, alignItems: "center", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => shareToWhatsApp(false)}>
              💬 {lang === "ar" ? "مشاركة" : "Share"}
            </button>
            {isAdmin && (
              <>
                <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => shareToWhatsApp(true)}>
                  ✨ {lang === "ar" ? "مشاركة كوصفة اليوم" : "Share as Today"}
                </button>
                <Link href={`/recipes/${recipe.id}/edit`} className="btn btn-ghost">{t(lang, "edit")}</Link>
                <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleting}>
                  {deleting ? "…" : lang === "ar" ? "🗑 حذف" : "🗑 Delete"}
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {displayDesc && (
          <>
            <div className="detail-desc-divider" />
            <p className="detail-desc">{displayDesc}</p>
          </>
        )}

        <div className="detail-meta">
          {catLabel && (
            <div className="meta-item">
              <span className="meta-label">{lang === "ar" ? "الفئة" : "Category"}</span>
              <span className="meta-value">{catIcon} {catLabel}</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="meta-item"><span className="meta-label">{t(lang, "prep")}</span><span className="meta-value">{recipe.prepTime} {minLabel}</span></div>
          <div className="meta-item"><span className="meta-label">{t(lang, "cook")}</span><span className="meta-value">{recipe.cookTime} {minLabel}</span></div>
          <div className="meta-item"><span className="meta-label">{t(lang, "min_total")}</span><span className="meta-value">{recipe.prepTime + recipe.cookTime} {minLabel}</span></div>
          <div className="meta-item"><span className="meta-label">{t(lang, "servings")}</span><span className="meta-value">{recipe.servings}</span></div>
          {displayCuisine && (
            <div className="meta-item"><span className="meta-label">{t(lang, "cuisine")}</span><span className="meta-value">{displayCuisine}</span></div>
          )}
        </div>

        {displayServedWith && (
          <div className="serve-note">
            <div className="serve-label">{lang === "ar" ? "يقدم مع" : "Served with"}</div>
            <div className="serve-value">{displayServedWith}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {displayHowToServe && (
          <div className="serve-note">
            <div className="serve-label">{lang === "ar" ? "طريقة التقديم" : "How to serve"}</div>
            <div className="serve-value">{displayHowToServe}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {tags.length > 0 && (
          <div className="chips" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
            {tags.map((tag) => (
              <span key={tag} className="chip active">{lang === "ar" ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {recipe.videoUrl && (
        <section className="video-section">
          <h2>{t(lang, "video")}</h2>
          {youtubeId ? (
            <div className="video-wrap">
              <iframe
                src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${youtubeId}`}
                title="Recipe video"
                allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                allowFullScreen
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <video
              src={recipe.videoUrl}
              controls
              preload="metadata"
              playsInline
              style={{
                width: "100%",
                borderRadius: "var(--radius)",
                border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                background: "#000",
                aspectRatio: "16 / 9",
                objectFit: "contain",
              }}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      <div className="detail-cols">
        <section className="detail-section">
          <h2>{t(lang, "ingredients")}</h2>
          {ingredients.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="helper">{t(lang, "no_ingredients")}</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="ing-list">
              {ingredients.map((i) => {
                const ingName = pick(lang, i.name, i.nameAr);
                const ingUnit = pick(lang, i.unit, i.unitAr);
                const ingPrep = pick(lang, i.preparation, i.preparationAr);
                return (
                  <li key={i.id}>
                    <span className="ing-amount">{[i.amount, ingUnit].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}</span>
                    <span className="ing-name">
                      {ingName}
                      {ingPrep && (
                        <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic" }}>
                          {lang === "ar" ? "، " : ", "}{ingPrep}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="detail-section">
          <h2>{t(lang, "instructions")}</h2>
          {instructions.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="helper">{t(lang, "no_instructions")}</p>
          ) : (
            <ol className="steps-list">
              {instructions.map((s) => (
                <li key={s.id}>
                  <div className="step-num">{s.stepNumber}</div>
                  <p>{pick(lang, s.text, s.textAr)}</p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}--- END FILE: src/components/RecipeDetailClient.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/RecipeForm.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import ImageUpload from "./ImageUpload";
import PickerDropdown from "./PickerDropdown";
import AutoTranslate from "./AutoTranslate";
import { DIETARY_TAGS } from "@/lib/dietary-tags";
import { parseIngredientsBulk } from "@/lib/parse-ingredients";
import { extractYouTubeId } from "@/lib/youtube";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { CUISINES, UNITS, PREPARATIONS } from "@/lib/pickers";
import { CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/categories";
import VideoUpload from "./VideoUpload";

type Ingredient = {
  name: string; nameAr: string; amount: string; unit: string; unitAr: string;
  preparation: string; preparationAr: string;
};
type Step = { text: string; textAr: string };

type InitialData = {
  id: number;
  name: string; nameAr: string | null;
  description: string | null; descriptionAr: string | null;
  cuisine: string | null; cuisineAr: string | null;
  category: string | null; categoryAr: string | null;
  servedWith: string | null; servedWithAr: string | null;
  howToServe: string | null; howToServeAr: string | null;
  prepTime: number; cookTime: number; servings: number;
  dietaryTags: string | null; imageUrl: string | null; videoUrl: string | null;
  ingredients: {
    name: string; nameAr: string | null; amount: string | null;
    unit: string | null; unitAr: string | null;
    preparation: string | null; preparationAr: string | null;
  }[];
  instructions: { text: string; textAr: string | null }[];
};

const TAG_AR: Record<string, string> = {
  Vegetarian: "نباتي", Vegan: "نباتي صرف", Pescatarian: "بيسكاتاري",
  "Gluten-Free": "خالٍ من الغلوتين", "Dairy-Free": "خالٍ من الألبان",
  "Nut-Free": "خالٍ من المكسرات", Halal: "حلال", Kosher: "كوشير",
  "Low-Carb": "قليل الكربوهيدرات", Keto: "كيتو", Paleo: "باليو",
  Meat: "لحوم", Chicken: "دجاج", Seafood: "مأكولات بحرية",
  Fish: "سمك", Lamb: "لحم غنم", Beef: "لحم بقري",
  Legumes: "بقوليات", Spicy: "حار", Quick: "سريع",
};

const emptyIngredient: Ingredient = {
  name: "", nameAr: "", amount: "", unit: "", unitAr: "", preparation: "", preparationAr: "",
};

export default function RecipeForm({ initial }: { initial?: InitialData }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isEdit = !!initial;

  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? "");
  const [nameAr, setNameAr] = useState(initial?.nameAr ?? "");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initial?.description ?? "");
  const [descriptionAr, setDescriptionAr] = useState(initial?.descriptionAr ?? "");
  const [cuisine, setCuisine] = useState(initial?.cuisine ?? "");
  const [cuisineAr, setCuisineAr] = useState(initial?.cuisineAr ?? "");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initial?.category ?? "");
  const [categoryAr, setCategoryAr] = useState(initial?.categoryAr ?? "");
  const [servedWith, setServedWith] = useState(initial?.servedWith ?? "");
  const [servedWithAr, setServedWithAr] = useState(initial?.servedWithAr ?? "");
  const [howToServe, setHowToServe] = useState(initial?.howToServe ?? "");
  const [howToServeAr, setHowToServeAr] = useState(initial?.howToServeAr ?? "");
  const [prepTime, setPrepTime] = useState(initial?.prepTime ? String(initial.prepTime) : "");
  const [cookTime, setCookTime] = useState(initial?.cookTime ? String(initial.cookTime) : "");
  const [servings, setServings] = useState(initial?.servings ? String(initial.servings) : "4");
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    if (!initial?.dietaryTags) return [];
    try { return JSON.parse(initial.dietaryTags); } catch { return []; }
  });
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(initial?.imageUrl ?? null);
  const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState(initial?.videoUrl ?? "");

  const [ingredientsList, setIngredientsList] = useState<Ingredient[]>(
    initial?.ingredients?.length
      ? initial.ingredients.map((i) => ({
          name: i.name, nameAr: i.nameAr ?? "", amount: i.amount ?? "",
          unit: i.unit ?? "", unitAr: i.unitAr ?? "",
          preparation: i.preparation ?? "", preparationAr: i.preparationAr ?? "",
        }))
      : [{ ...emptyIngredient }]
  );

  const [steps, setSteps] = useState<Step[]>(
    initial?.instructions?.length
      ? initial.instructions.map((s) => ({ text: s.text, textAr: s.textAr ?? "" }))
      : [{ text: "", textAr: "" }]
  );

  const [bulkIngredients, setBulkIngredients] = useState("");
  const [bulkSteps, setBulkSteps] = useState("");
  const [showBulkIng, setShowBulkIng] = useState(false);
  const [showBulkStep, setShowBulkStep] = useState(false);

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const totalTime = useMemo(() => (parseInt(prepTime) || 0) + (parseInt(cookTime) || 0), [prepTime, cookTime]);
  const videoPreviewId = useMemo(() => extractYouTubeId(videoUrl), [videoUrl]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      if (name || description) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ""; }
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", handler);
  }, [name, description]);

  function toggleTag(tag: string) {
    setTags((tags) => (tags.includes(tag) ? tags.filter((x) => x !== tag) : [...tags, tag]));
  }
  function updateIngredient(i: number, patch: Partial<Ingredient>) {
    setIngredientsList((list) => list.map((ing, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...ing, ...patch } : ing)));
  }
  function addIngredient() { setIngredientsList((l) => [...l, { ...emptyIngredient }]); }
  function removeIngredient(i: number) {
    setIngredientsList((l) => (l.length === 1 ? l : l.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i)));
  }
  function applyBulkIngredients() {
    const parsed = parseIngredientsBulk(bulkIngredients);
    if (parsed.length === 0) return;
    setIngredientsList((prev) => {
      const cleaned = prev.filter((i) => i.name.trim() || i.nameAr.trim() || i.amount.trim());
      return [...cleaned, ...parsed.map((p) => ({
        name: p.name, nameAr: "", amount: p.amount, unit: p.unit,
        unitAr: "", preparation: p.preparation, preparationAr: "",
      }))];
    });
    setBulkIngredients("");
    setShowBulkIng(false);
  }
  function updateStep(i: number, patch: Partial<Step>) {
    setSteps((s) => s.map((st, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...st, ...patch } : st)));
  }
  function addStep() { setSteps((s) => [...s, { text: "", textAr: "" }]); }
  function removeStep(i: number) {
    setSteps((s) => (s.length === 1 ? s : s.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i)));
  }
  function applyBulkSteps() {
    const lines = bulkSteps.split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((l) => l.replace(/^[\s•·●○▪▫■□◆◇♦→*\-–—]+/, "").trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (lines.length === 0) return;
    setSteps((prev) => {
      const cleaned = prev.filter((s) => s.text.trim() || s.textAr.trim());
      return [...cleaned, ...lines.map((text) => ({ text, textAr: "" }))];
    });
    setBulkSteps("");
    setShowBulkStep(false);
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    if (!name.trim() && !nameAr.trim()) { setError(t(lang, "name_required")); return; }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const url = isEdit ? `/api/recipes/${initial!.id}` : "/api/recipes";
      const method = isEdit ? "PUT" : "POST";
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: name.trim() || nameAr.trim(), nameAr,
          description, descriptionAr,
          cuisine, cuisineAr,
          category, categoryAr,
          servedWith, servedWithAr,
          howToServe, howToServeAr,
          prepTime: parseInt(prepTime) || 0,
          cookTime: parseInt(cookTime) || 0,
          servings: parseInt(servings) || 1,
          dietaryTags: tags, imageUrl,
          videoUrl: videoUrl.trim() || null,
          ingredients: ingredientsList.filter((i) => i.name.trim() || i.nameAr.trim()),
          instructions: steps.filter((s) => s.text.trim() || s.textAr.trim()),
        }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(data.error || "Failed to save recipe");
      }

      setToast(isEdit ? t(lang, "recipe_updated") : t(lang, "recipe_saved"));
      const dest = isEdit ? `/recipes/${initial!.id}` : "/";
      setTimeout(() => router.push(dest), 700);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Something went wrong");
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="form-card" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      {error && (
        <div style={{ background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", padding: "12px 16px", borderRadius: 10, marginBottom: 24, fontSize: 13 }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="form-section">
        <h2>{t(lang, "basics")}</h2>

        <div className="field-pair">
          <div className="field">
            <label>Recipe name (EN)</label>
            <input type="text" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} dir="ltr" />
          </div>
          <div className="field">
            <label>اسم الوصفة (AR)</label>
            <input type="text" value={nameAr} onChange={(e) => setNameAr(e.target.value)} dir="rtl" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="field-pair">
          <div className="field">
            <label>Description (EN)</label>

    <div className="video-option">
      <div className="video-option-label">
        <span className="video-option-num">1</span>

                <input className="cell-name" type="text" placeholder={t(lang, "ingredient")} value={ing.name} onChange={(e) => updateIngredient(i, { name: e.target.value })} dir="ltr" />
                <div className="input-with-tools">
                  <input className="cell-prep" type="text" placeholder={t(lang, "prep_short")} value={ing.preparation} onChange={(e) => updateIngredient(i, { preparation: e.target.value })} dir="ltr" />
                  <AutoTranslate kind="prep" value={ing.preparation} sourceIsAr={false} onTranslated={(v) => updateIngredient(i, { preparationAr: v })} />

                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="ing-stack-divider" />

                  <AutoTranslate kind="unit" value={ing.unitAr} sourceIsAr={true} onTranslated={(v) => updateIngredient(i, { unit: v })} />

                <div className="input-with-tools">

                  <AutoTranslate kind="prep" value={ing.preparationAr} sourceIsAr={true} onTranslated={(v) => updateIngredient(i, { preparation: v })} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 12 }}>
          <button type="button" className="add-row" onClick={addIngredient}>{t(lang, "add_ingredient")}</button>
          <button type="button" className="add-row" onClick={() => setShowBulkIng((v) => !v)} style={{ maxWidth: 220 }}>{t(lang, "bulk_paste")}</button>
        </div>
        {showBulkIng && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
            <textarea placeholder={t(lang, "bulk_ing_ph")} value={bulkIngredients} onChange={(e) => setBulkIngredients(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", minHeight: 140, padding: 12, border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, background: "var(--input-bg)", fontSize: 13 }} />
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={applyBulkIngredients}>{t(lang, "parse_add")}</button>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => { setBulkIngredients(""); setShowBulkIng(false); }}>{t(lang, "cancel")}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="form-section">
        <h2>{t(lang, "instructions")}</h2>
        <div className="step-stack-list">
          {steps.map((st, i) => (
            <div className="step-stack" key={i}>
              <div className="step-stack-head">
                <span className="step-stack-num">{i + 1}</span>
                <span className="step-stack-label">{lang === "ar" ? "إنجليزي" : "English"}</span>
                <button type="button" className="icon-btn" onClick={() => removeStep(i)} disabled={steps.length === 1}>✕</button>
              </div>
              <textarea placeholder={t(lang, "step_en")} value={st.text} onChange={(e) => updateStep(i, { text: e.target.value })} dir="ltr" />
              <div className="step-stack-divider" />
              <div className="step-stack-ar">
                <span className="ing-stack-tag">AR</span>
                <textarea placeholder={t(lang, "step_ar")} value={st.textAr} onChange={(e) => updateStep(i, { textAr: e.target.value })} dir="rtl" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 12 }}>
          <button type="button" className="add-row" onClick={addStep}>{t(lang, "add_step")}</button>
          <button type="button" className="add-row" onClick={() => setShowBulkStep((v) => !v)} style={{ maxWidth: 220 }}>{t(lang, "bulk_paste")}</button>
        </div>
        {showBulkStep && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
            <textarea placeholder={t(lang, "bulk_step_ph")} value={bulkSteps} onChange={(e) => setBulkSteps(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", minHeight: 140, padding: 12, border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, background: "var(--input-bg)", fontSize: 13 }} />
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={applyBulkSteps}>{t(lang, "parse_add")}</button>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => { setBulkSteps(""); setShowBulkStep(false); }}>{t(lang, "cancel")}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <div className="form-actions">
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => router.push(isEdit ? `/recipes/${initial!.id}` : "/")} disabled={submitting}>{t(lang, "cancel")}</button>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? t(lang, "saving") : isEdit ? t(lang, "save_changes") : t(lang, "save")}
        </button>
      </div>

      {toast && <div className="toast">{toast}</div>}
    </form>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/ThemeProvider.tsx ---
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

type Theme = "dark" | "light";
type Ctx = { theme: Theme; toggle: () => void; setTheme: (t: Theme) => void };

const ThemeContext = createContext<Ctx>({
  theme: "dark",
  toggle: () => {},
  setTheme: () => {},
});

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [theme, setThemeState] = useState<Theme>("dark");

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem("theme") as Theme | null;
    if (stored === "dark" || stored === "light") {
      setThemeState(stored);
    } else {
      // Follow system preference on first visit
      const prefersLight = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches;
      setThemeState(prefersLight ? "light" : "dark");
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
    localStorage.setItem("theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  function setTheme(t: Theme) {
    setThemeState(t);
  }

  function toggle() {
    setThemeState((prev) => (prev === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"));
  }

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggle, setTheme }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  return useContext(ThemeContext);
}
--- END FILE: src/components/ThemeProvider.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/VideoUpload.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  onUploaded: (publicUrl: string) => void;
  initialUrl?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export default function VideoUpload({ onUploaded, initialUrl, disabled }: Props) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(initialUrl ?? null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleFile(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    if (!file.type.startsWith("video/")) {
      setError("Please select a video file (mp4, webm, mov)");
      return;
    }

    setError(null);
    setUploading(true);
    setProgress(0);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/upload", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ filename: file.name, contentType: file.type }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to get upload URL");
      const { uploadUrl, key } = await res.json();

      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        xhr.open("PUT", uploadUrl);
        xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", file.type);

        xhr.upload.onprogress = (ev) => {
          if (ev.lengthComputable) {
            setProgress(Math.round((ev.loaded / ev.total) * 100));
          }
        };

        xhr.onload = () => {
          if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) resolve();
          else reject(new Error(`Upload failed: ${xhr.status}`));
        };
        xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Network error"));
        xhr.send(file);
      });

      const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_R2_PUBLIC_URL;
      const publicUrl = `${base}/${key}`;

      setPreview(publicUrl);
      onUploaded(publicUrl);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  function clearVideo() {
    setPreview(null);
    onUploaded("");
    setError(null);
    setProgress(0);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <input
        type="file"
        accept="video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime"
        onChange={handleFile}
        disabled={uploading || disabled}
        style={{ display: "block", width: "100%", fontSize: 13 }}
      />

      {uploading && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
          <div style={{ height: 6, borderRadius: 3, background: "var(--line)", overflow: "hidden" }}>
            <div style={{
              height: "100%", width: `${progress}%`, background: "var(--accent)",
              transition: "width 0.2s ease",
            }} />
          </div>
          <p className="helper" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>Uploading… {progress}%</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && <p style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 12, marginTop: 8 }}>{error}</p>}

      {preview && !uploading && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
          <video
            src={preview}
            controls
            preload="metadata"
            playsInline
            style={{ width: "100%", borderRadius: 10, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "#000", aspectRatio: "16 / 9", objectFit: "contain" }}
          />
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8, marginTop: 8, alignItems: "center" }}>
            <p className="helper" style={{ wordBreak: "break-all", margin: 0, flex: 1 }}>{preview}</p>
            <button type="button" className="icon-btn" onClick={clearVideo} title="Remove video">✕</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/components/WhatsAppSender.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";

type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
};

export default function WhatsAppSender() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Recipe 1 State
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string>("");
  const [showDropdown, setShowDropdown] = useState(false);

  // Recipe 2 State
  const [searchQuery2, setSearchQuery2] = useState("");
  const [selectedId2, setSelectedId2] = useState<string>("");
  const [showDropdown2, setShowDropdown2] = useState(false);

  // Message Fields
  const [greeting, setGreeting] = useState("*Thank you for joining dinner yesterday*");
  const [line1, setLine1] = useState("We were honored by your presence!");
  const [line2, setLine2] = useState("We hope you enjoyed:");
  const [linkText, setLinkText] = useState("View the full recipe here:");
  const [includeToday, setIncludeToday] = useState(false);
  const [signature, setSignature] = useState("- Hicham");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/recipes")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setRecipes(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  // Filter for Recipe 1
  const filteredRecipes = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return recipes;
    const lower = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return recipes.filter(r => r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || (r.nameAr && r.nameAr.includes(searchQuery)));
  }, [recipes, searchQuery]);

  // Filter for Recipe 2
  const filteredRecipes2 = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery2.trim()) return recipes;
    const lower = searchQuery2.toLowerCase();
    return recipes.filter(r => r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || (r.nameAr && r.nameAr.includes(searchQuery2)));
  }, [recipes, searchQuery2]);

  const selectedRecipe = recipes.find((r) => r.id.toString() === selectedId);
  const recipeName = selectedRecipe ? (lang === "ar" && selectedRecipe.nameAr ? selectedRecipe.nameAr : selectedRecipe.name) : "";
  
  const selectedRecipe2 = recipes.find((r) => r.id.toString() === selectedId2);
  const recipeName2 = selectedRecipe2 ? (lang === "ar" && selectedRecipe2.nameAr ? selectedRecipe2.nameAr : selectedRecipe2.name) : "";
  
  const baseUrl = "https://food-menu-app-xxww.onrender.com";
  const suffix = includeToday ? "?today" : "";
  
  const recipeLink = selectedId ? `${baseUrl}/recipes/${selectedId}${suffix}` : "";
  const recipeLink2 = selectedId2 ? `${baseUrl}/recipes/${selectedId2}${suffix}` : "";

  // Build final message
  const messageParts = [
    greeting,
    line1,
    line2,
    // Recipe 1
    selectedId && recipeName ? `*${recipeName}*` : "",
    selectedId && recipeLink ? recipeLink : "",
    // Recipe 2 (if selected)
    selectedId2 && recipeName2 ? `*${recipeName2}*` : "",
    selectedId2 && recipeLink2 ? recipeLink2 : "",
    // Link Text (ONLY show if NO recipes are selected)
    (!selectedId && !selectedId2) ? linkText : "",
    signature
  ];

  // Combine and clean up empty lines
  const finalMessage = messageParts
    .filter(part => part && part.trim() !== "")
    .join("\n\n")
    .replace(/^\uFEFF/, "")
    .replace(/\uFEFF/g, "");

  function openWhatsApp() {
    if (!finalMessage.trim()) return;
    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(finalMessage);
    window.open(`https://wa.me/?text=${encoded}`, "_blank");
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "إرسال واتساب" : "Send WhatsApp"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {lang === "ar" ? "أرسل رسالة مخصصة مع روابط الوصفات" : "Send a custom message with recipe links"}
      </p>

      <div className="form-card" style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
        
        {/* Recipe 1 Selector */}
        <div className="field" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <label>Recipe 1 (Optional)</label>
          {selectedRecipe ? (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "10px", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--accent)", background: "var(--accent-soft)" }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{recipeName}</span>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setSelectedId(""); setSearchQuery(""); }} style={{ background: "transparent", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: "bold" }}>✕</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <input 
              type="text" placeholder="Type to search..." value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => { setSearchQuery(e.target.value); setShowDropdown(true); }}
              onFocus={() => setShowDropdown(true)}
              style={{ width: "100%", padding: "10px", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)" }}
            />
          )}
          {showDropdown && !selectedRecipe && (
            <>
              <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 10 }} onClick={() => setShowDropdown(false)} />
              <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: "100%", left: 0, right: 0, maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", background: "var(--surface-solid)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, zIndex: 20, marginTop: 4, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)" }}>
                {filteredRecipes.length === 0 ? <div style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>No recipes found</div> : filteredRecipes.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { setSelectedId(r.id.toString()); setShowDropdown(false); }} style={{ padding: "10px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", fontSize: 14, color: "var(--ink)" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"} onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
                    {lang === "ar" && r.nameAr ? r.nameAr : r.name}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Recipe 2 Selector */}
        <div className="field" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <label>Recipe 2 (Optional)</label>
          {selectedRecipe2 ? (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "10px", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--accent)", background: "var(--accent-soft)" }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{recipeName2}</span>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setSelectedId2(""); setSearchQuery2(""); }} style={{ background: "transparent", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: "bold" }}>✕</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <input 
              type="text" placeholder="Type to search..." value={searchQuery2}
              onChange={(e) => { setSearchQuery2(e.target.value); setShowDropdown2(true); }}
              onFocus={() => setShowDropdown2(true)}
              style={{ width: "100%", padding: "10px", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)" }}
            />
          )}
          {showDropdown2 && !selectedRecipe2 && (
            <>
              <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 10 }} onClick={() => setShowDropdown2(false)} />
              <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: "100%", left: 0, right: 0, maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", background: "var(--surface-solid)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, zIndex: 20, marginTop: 4, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)" }}>
                {filteredRecipes2.length === 0 ? <div style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>No recipes found</div> : filteredRecipes2.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { setSelectedId2(r.id.toString()); setShowDropdown2(false); }} style={{ padding: "10px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", fontSize: 14, color: "var(--ink)" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"} onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
                    {lang === "ar" && r.nameAr ? r.nameAr : r.name}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Message Inputs */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, background: "var(--bg)", padding: 16, borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
          <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
            <label>Greeting / Title</label>
            <input type="text" value={greeting} onChange={(e) => setGreeting(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          </div>
          <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
            <label>Line 1</label>
            <input type="text" value={line1} onChange={(e) => setLine1(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          </div>
          <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
            <label>Line 2</label>
            <input type="text" value={line2} onChange={(e) => setLine2(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          </div>
          
          <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
            <label>Link Text (Only shows if no recipe is selected)</label>
            <input type="text" value={linkText} onChange={(e) => setLinkText(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          </div>

          <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
            <label>Signature / Footer</label>
            <input type="text" value={signature} onChange={(e) => setSignature(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. - Hicham" style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <input type="checkbox" id="today-check" checked={includeToday} onChange={(e) => setIncludeToday(e.target.checked)} />
            <label htmlFor="today-check" style={{ margin: 0, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 13 }}>Add <code>?today</code> to links</label>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Live Preview */}
        <div>
          <label style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)" }}>
            {lang === "ar" ? "معاينة حية" : "Live Preview"}
          </label>
          <div style={{ background: "#e5ddd5", padding: 16, borderRadius: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ background: "#dcf8c6", padding: 12, borderRadius: 10, maxWidth: "90%", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", fontSize: 14, color: "#000", boxShadow: "0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)" }}>
              {finalMessage || "Type a message..."}
              
              {selectedRecipe && recipeLink && (
                <div style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)", borderRadius: 8, marginTop: 8, overflow: "hidden", borderLeft: "3px solid #25D366" }}>
                  {selectedRecipe.imageUrl && <img src={selectedRecipe.imageUrl} alt="Preview" style={{ width: "100%", height: 120, objectFit: "cover" }} />}
                  <div style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 2 }}>{recipeName}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#666", wordBreak: "break-all" }}>{recipeLink}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {selectedRecipe2 && recipeLink2 && (
                <div style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)", borderRadius: 8, marginTop: 8, overflow: "hidden", borderLeft: "3px solid #25D366" }}>
                  {selectedRecipe2.imageUrl && <img src={selectedRecipe2.imageUrl} alt="Preview" style={{ width: "100%", height: 120, objectFit: "cover" }} />}
                  <div style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 2 }}>{recipeName2}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#666", wordBreak: "break-all" }}>{recipeLink2}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button onClick={openWhatsApp} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ width: "100%", padding: 14, fontSize: 16, background: "#25D366", borderColor: "#25D366", color: "#fff" }} disabled={!finalMessage.trim()}>
          {lang === "ar" ? "فتح واتساب" : "Open WhatsApp"}
        </button>

      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/WhatsAppSender.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/db/index.ts ---
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/libsql";
import { createClient } from "@libsql/client";
import * as schema from "./schema";

let _db: ReturnType<typeof drizzle> | null = null;

export function getDb() {
  if (!_db) {
    const url = process.env.TURSO_DATABASE_URL;
    if (!url) throw new Error("TURSO_DATABASE_URL is not set");
    const client = createClient({
      url,
      authToken: process.env.TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN,
    });
    _db = drizzle(client, { schema });
  }
  return _db;
}
--- END FILE: src/db/index.ts ---

--- FILE: src/db/schema.ts ---
import { sqliteTable, text, integer } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

export const recipes = sqliteTable("recipes", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  nameAr: text("name_ar"),
  description: text("description"),
  descriptionAr: text("description_ar"),
  prepTime: integer("prep_time").notNull(),
  cookTime: integer("cook_time").notNull(),
  servings: integer("servings").notNull(),
  cuisine: text("cuisine"),
  cuisineAr: text("cuisine_ar"),
  category: text("category"),
  categoryAr: text("category_ar"),
  servedWith: text("served_with"),
  servedWithAr: text("served_with_ar"),
  howToServe: text("how_to_serve"),
  howToServeAr: text("how_to_serve_ar"),
  dietaryTags: text("dietary_tags"),
  imageUrl: text("image_url"),
  videoUrl: text("video_url"),
  createdAt: text("created_at").default(sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`),
});

export const ingredients = sqliteTable("ingredients", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
  recipeId: integer("recipe_id")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => recipes.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  nameAr: text("name_ar"),
  amount: text("amount"),
  unit: text("unit"),
  unitAr: text("unit_ar"),
  preparation: text("preparation"),
  preparationAr: text("preparation_ar"),
});

export const instructions = sqliteTable("instructions", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
  recipeId: integer("recipe_id")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => recipes.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  stepNumber: integer("step_number").notNull(),
  text: text("text").notNull(),
  textAr: text("text_ar"),
});

export const pushSubscriptions = sqliteTable("push_subscriptions", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
  endpoint: text("endpoint").notNull().unique(),
  p256dh: text("p256dh").notNull(),
  auth: text("auth").notNull(),
  deviceName: text("device_name"),
  createdAt: text("created_at").default(sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`),
});

export const notifications = sqliteTable("notifications", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  body: text("body").notNull(),
  url: text("url"),
  previewImage: text("preview_image"),
  previewTitle: text("preview_title"),
  createdAt: text("created_at").default(sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`),
});

export const settings = sqliteTable("settings", {
  key: text("key").primaryKey(),
  value: text("value").notNull(),
});
--- END FILE: src/db/schema.ts ---

--- FILE: src/lib/auth.ts ---
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import crypto from "crypto";

const COOKIE = "admin_session";
const MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 7; // 7 days

function secret() {
  return process.env.ADMIN_SESSION_SECRET || "dev-insecure-secret";
}

function sign(value: string) {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret()).update(value).digest("hex");
}

export function createToken(): string {
  const payload = String(Date.now());
  return `${payload}.${sign(payload)}`;
}

export function verifyToken(token: string | undefined): boolean {
  if (!token) return false;
  const [payload, sig] = token.split(".");
  if (!payload || !sig) return false;
  const expected = sign(payload);
  if (sig.length !== expected.length) return false;
  if (!crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(sig), Buffer.from(expected))) return false;
  const ts = Number(payload);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) return false;
  if (Date.now() - ts > MAX_AGE * 1000) return false;
  return true;
}

export async function isAdmin(): Promise<boolean> {
  // This tells Next.js 16 this function requires a live request
  await connection();
  
  const store = await cookies();
  return verifyToken(store.get(COOKIE)?.value);
}

export const COOKIE_NAME = COOKIE;
export const COOKIE_MAX_AGE = MAX_AGE;
--- END FILE: src/lib/auth.ts ---

export type Category = {
  value: string;
  valueAr: string;
  icon: string;
};

export const CATEGORIES: Category[] = [
  { value: "Main Dish",     valueAr: "طبق رئيسي",     icon: "🍽️" },
  { value: "Stew",          valueAr: "يخنة",           icon: "🍲" },
  { value: "Grill",         valueAr: "مشاوي",          icon: "🔥" },
  { value: "Hot Mezze",     valueAr: "مازة ساخنة",     icon: "🥟" },
  { value: "Cold Mezze",    valueAr: "مازة باردة",     icon: "🥗" },
  { value: "Side",          valueAr: "طبق جانبي",     icon: "🥗" },
  { value: "Appetizer",     valueAr: "مقبلات",         icon: "🥟" },
  { value: "Soup",          valueAr: "شوربة",          icon: "🥣" },
  { value: "Salad",         valueAr: "سلطة",           icon: "🥬" },
  { value: "Bread",         valueAr: "خبز",            icon: "🥖" },
  { value: "Pastry",        valueAr: "معجنات",         icon: "🥐" },
  { value: "Rice",          valueAr: "أطباق الأرز",    icon: "🍚" },
  { value: "Pasta",         valueAr: "معكرونة",        icon: "🍝" },
  { value: "Breakfast",     valueAr: "فطور",           icon: "🌅" },
  { value: "Snack",         valueAr: "وجبة خفيفة",    icon: "🍿" },
  { value: "Dessert",       valueAr: "حلويات",         icon: "🍰" },
  { value: "Drink",         valueAr: "مشروب",          icon: "🥤" },
  { value: "Joke Recipe",   valueAr: "وصفة مزحة",      icon: "😂" },
];

export function categoryIcon(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  const c = CATEGORIES.find(
    (x) => x.value === value || x.valueAr === value
  );
  return c ? c.icon : "";
}

export function categoryLabel(
  value: string | null | undefined,
  lang: "en" | "ar"
): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  const c = CATEGORIES.find(
    (x) => x.value === value || x.valueAr === value
  );
  if (!c) return value;
  return lang === "ar" ? c.valueAr : c.value;
}

type FlagInfo = { emoji: string; code: string }; // code = ISO 3166-1 alpha-2, lowercase

const CUISINE_FLAGS: Record<string, FlagInfo> = {
  // Middle East
  Lebanese:      { emoji: "🇱🇧", code: "lb" },
  Syrian:        { emoji: "🇸🇾", code: "sy" },

const ARABIC_TO_FLAG: Record<string, FlagInfo> = {
  "لبناني":    { emoji: "🇱🇧", code: "lb" },

  "مصري":      { emoji: "🇪🇬", code: "eg" },

  "يمني":      { emoji: "🇾🇪", code: "ye" },

  "مغربي":     { emoji: "🇲🇦", code: "ma" },
  "تونسي":     { emoji: "🇹🇳", code: "tn" },
  "جزائري":    { emoji: "🇩🇿", code: "dz" },
  "تركي":      { emoji: "🇹🇷", code: "tr" },
  "يوناني":    { emoji: "🇬🇷", code: "gr" },
  "إيطالي":    { emoji: "🇮🇹", code: "it" },
  "إسباني":    { emoji: "🇪🇸", code: "es" },
  "فرنسي":     { emoji: "🇫🇷", code: "fr" },
  "ألماني":    { emoji: "🇩🇪", code: "de" },
  "بريطاني":   { emoji: "🇬🇧", code: "gb" },
  "هندي":      { emoji: "🇮🇳", code: "in" },
  "باكستاني":  { emoji: "🇵🇰", code: "pk" },
  "صيني":      { emoji: "🇨🇳", code: "cn" },
  "ياباني":    { emoji: "🇯🇵", code: "jp" },
  "كوري":      { emoji: "🇰🇷", code: "kr" },
  "تايلندي":   { emoji: "🇹🇭", code: "th" },
  "فيتنامي":   { emoji: "🇻🇳", code: "vn" },
  "إندونيسي":  { emoji: "🇮🇩", code: "id" },
  "فلبيني":    { emoji: "🇵🇭", code: "ph" },
  "مكسيكي":    { emoji: "🇲🇽", code: "mx" },
  "أمريكي":    { emoji: "🇺🇸", code: "us" },
  "برازيلي":   { emoji: "🇧🇷", code: "br" },
  "بيروفي":    { emoji: "🇵🇪", code: "pe" },
};

export function cuisineFlagInfo(cuisine: string | null | undefined): FlagInfo | null {
  if (!cuisine) return null;
  const trimmed = cuisine.trim();
  return CUISINE_FLAGS[trimmed] || ARABIC_TO_FLAG[trimmed] || null;
}

/** SVG URL from flagcdn (works everywhere including Windows) */
export function cuisineFlagUrl(cuisine: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const info = cuisineFlagInfo(cuisine);
  return info ? `https://flagcdn.com/${info.code}.svg` : null;
}

/** Emoji fallback (renders on iOS/macOS/Android, shows letters on Windows) */
export function cuisineFlag(cuisine: string | null | undefined): string {
  const info = cuisineFlagInfo(cuisine);
  return info?.emoji || "";
}

export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof translations.en;

export function t(lang: Lang, key: TranslationKey): string {
  return translations[lang][key] ?? translations.en[key] ?? key;
}

export function pick(
  lang: Lang,
  en: string | null | undefined,
  ar: string | null | undefined
): string {
  if (lang === "ar") return (ar && ar.trim()) || en || "";
  return (en && en.trim()) || ar || "";
}

--- FILE: src/lib/parse-ingredients.ts ---
const UNITS = [
  "tablespoons", "tablespoon", "tbsp", "tbs",
  "teaspoons", "teaspoon", "tsp",
  "spoons", "spoon", "spn",
  "cups", "cup",
  "grams", "gram", "gr", "g",
  "kilograms", "kilogram", "kg",
  "milliliters", "milliliter", "ml",
  "liters", "liter", "l",
  "ounces", "ounce", "oz",
  "pounds", "pound", "lb", "lbs",
  "cloves", "clove",
  "slices", "slice",
  "cans", "can",
  "pinches", "pinch",
  "bunches", "bunch",
  "stalks", "stalk",
  "sprigs", "sprig",
  "heads", "head",
  "dashes", "dash",
  "handfuls", "handful",
  "portions", "portion",
  "servings", "serving",
  "pieces", "piece",
  "packs", "pack",
  "pockets", "pocket",
  "bottles", "bottle",
  "drops", "drop",
];

const AMOUNT_RE = /^(\d+\s*[-–]\s*\d+|\d+\/\d+|\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?|[½¼¾⅓⅔⅛⅜⅝⅞])/;
const BULLET_RE = /^[\s•·●○▪▫■□◆◇♦→*\-–—]+/;

export type ParsedIngredient = {
  amount: string;
  unit: string;
  name: string;
  preparation: string;
};

export function parseIngredientLine(raw: string): ParsedIngredient | null {
  let line = raw.replace(BULLET_RE, "").trim();
  if (!line) return null;

  let preparation = "";

  const parenMatch = line.match(/\s*\(([^)]+)\)\s*$/);
  if (parenMatch) {
    preparation = parenMatch[1].trim();
    line = line.slice(0, parenMatch.index).trim();
  } else {
    const commaMatch = line.match(/,\s*([a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z\s-]{2,30})$/);
    if (commaMatch) {
      preparation = commaMatch[1].trim();
      line = line.slice(0, commaMatch.index).trim();
    }
  }

  let rest = line;
  let amount = "";
  let unit = "";

  const amtMatch = rest.match(AMOUNT_RE);
  if (amtMatch) {
    amount = amtMatch[0].replace(/\s+/g, "");
    rest = rest.slice(amtMatch[0].length).trim();
  }

  if (amount) {
    const lower = rest.toLowerCase();
    for (const u of UNITS) {
      if (
        lower === u ||
        lower.startsWith(u + " ") ||
        lower.startsWith(u + ".")
      ) {
        unit = rest.slice(0, u.length);
        rest = rest.slice(u.length).replace(/^[.\s]+/, "").trim();
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  const name = rest.trim();
  if (!name) return null;
  return { amount, unit, name, preparation };
}

export function parseIngredientsBulk(input: string): ParsedIngredient[] {
  return input
    .split(/\r?\n/)
    .map(parseIngredientLine)
    .filter((x): x is ParsedIngredient => x !== null);
}
--- END FILE: src/lib/parse-ingredients.ts ---

import { S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";

export const r2 = new S3Client({
  region: "auto",
  endpoint: `https://${process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
  credentials: {
    accessKeyId: process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID!,
    secretAccessKey: process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY!,
  },
  requestChecksumCalculation: "WHEN_REQUIRED",
  responseChecksumValidation: "WHEN_REQUIRED",
});

export const R2_BUCKET = process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME!;
export const R2_PUBLIC_URL = process.env.R2_PUBLIC_URL!;

--- FILE: src/lib/youtube.ts ---
/**
 * Accepts any of:
 *   - https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XXXXXXXXXXX
 *   - https://youtu.be/XXXXXXXXXXX
 *   - https://www.youtube.com/embed/XXXXXXXXXXX
 *   - https://www.youtube.com/shorts/XXXXXXXXXXX
 *   - XXXXXXXXXXX (bare 11-char id)
 * Returns the 11-char video id or null.
 */
export function extractYouTubeId(input: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!input) return null;
  const s = input.trim();
  if (!s) return null;

  // Bare id
  if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(s)) return s;

  try {
    const url = new URL(s);
    const host = url.hostname.replace(/^www\./, "");

    if (host === "youtu.be") {
      const id = url.pathname.slice(1).split("/")[0];
      return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(id) ? id : null;
    }

    if (host.endsWith("youtube.com")) {
      // watch?v=
      const v = url.searchParams.get("v");
      if (v && /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(v)) return v;

      // /embed/ID, /shorts/ID, /v/ID
      const parts = url.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
      if (parts.length >= 2) {
        const candidate = parts[1];
        if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(candidate)) return candidate;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // not a URL
  }

  return null;
}
